We study whether five LLMs can generate such world models as partially observable stochastic games and whether mechanical validity is sufficient to establish that the resulting model faithfully represents the intended decision problem. We introduce a benchmark of 44 scenarios spanning game, situational, and cultural settings and evaluate generated world models along three dimensions: mechanical validity, reference-relative structural agreement, and semantic criteria. Across three empirical tracks, we analyze 410 first-pass generations. In the reference-backed game track, 134/135 generated world models pass a strict mechanical audit, yet mean permutation-invariant structural F1 is only 0.366, with just 28/134 mechanically valid models exactly matching the authored reference. In the cultural track, explicitly naming the country changes the generated structural representation for most LLMs while producing only small changes in automated semantic scores. These results show that executability is a necessary but insufficient criterion for LLM-generated world models: models that are mechanically valid can still differ substantially in the structure used by downstream neuro-symbolic reasoning and planning.

</details>


### [102] [Re-Search: Dissecting LLM Ideation Through a Two-Player Questioning Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14487)
*Muhan Gao,Hangxiao Zhu,Xinqi Su,Yu Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Re-Search以"二十问"范式评测LLM的研究构思搜索过程，发现进展集中在前30问、瓶颈在生成而非判断，建议自动科研系统借助外部想法与问题丰富搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方式掩盖了研究想法迭代搜索的过程，难以理解LLM如何探索研究想法空间及其进步瓶颈所在。

Method: 提出Re-Search，将"二十问"游戏格式迁移到研究构思：仅给定已发表论文的研究问题，模型需通过提出是/否问题获得二元反馈，重新发现论文核心想法；基于200篇ICML 2026论文构建，隔离构思与执行。开展对候选问题与假设空间的受控干预实验，并分析构思轨迹。

Result: 六个近期LLM中，几乎所有进展发生在前30个问题（共100个）内，此后多数模型进入平台期；干预表明瓶颈在于"生成"而非"判断"——模型能识别好的想法或问题，却难以自主产出；失败模式分为过度开发单一方向与过度探索过多方向两类。

Conclusion: 对自动科研系统的设计启示：由于模型具备对候选的判别力而缺乏生成力，向搜索中注入外部来源的想法与问题，可能比提升单一模型的生成能力更有效。

Abstract: Research ideation is iterative: researchers refine their ideas step by step based on feedback. Yet existing evaluations obscure this search process, making it difficult to understand how LLMs explore the space of research ideas and what limits their progress. We introduce Re-Search, which adapts the format of Twenty Questions to research ideation: given only the research problem of a published paper, a model must rediscover the paper's core idea by asking yes-or-no questions. Built from 200 ICML 2026 papers, Re-Search tracks how a model's idea evolves as it asks questions and receives binary oracle feedback, isolating ideation from execution. Across six recent LLMs, nearly all progress happens within the first 30 of 100 questions, which largely decide how the game ends; after that, most models plateau. Through controlled interventions on the candidate questions and the hypothesis space, we find that the bottleneck is generation, not judgment: models recognize a good idea or a good question when one is offered, but often cannot produce it on their own. We further analyze the ideation traces and find that models fail by either over-exploiting one direction or over-exploring too many. These findings point to concrete design principles for autoresearch systems: since models can judge candidates they cannot produce, enriching the search with externally sourced ideas and questions may be more effective than improving any single model's generation ability.

</details>


### [103] [ReTrace: Distilling Tool-Using Agents into Planner-Free Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14492)
*Kaiyuan Hou,Yang Ping Kuo,Indranil Bhattacharya,Rohith Nama,Arushi Gupta,Carlos D. Castillo,Prathap Ramachandra,Xiaofan Jiang,Francesco Carbone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTrace 将 SOP 与少量 agent 运行日志转化为可复用工作流，由代码而非 LLM 决定下一步，在保持接近 agent 成功率的同时大幅降低延迟与成本，并支持跨平台编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 能按自然语言 SOP 执行任务，但每一步都调用 LLM 会带来规模化下的高延迟与高成本；需要把重复执行的 SOP 固化为可复用、低开销的工作流。

Method: 利用 agent 每次工具调用时写下的推理说明（其中隐含采取该动作的条件），提取每一步背后的条件；跨多次运行合并为紧凑的规则文档；再由规则文档生成可执行工作流。仅最后一步依平台而定，故规则文档可编译到不同编排平台。另采用基于带标签训练失败的修复（repair）迭代。

Result: 生产平台 79 个流程：ReTrace 完成率 93.8%（agent 96.8%），单任务成本低 24%。τ²-Bench：任务成功率 75.8%（agent 80.6%），运行时间 1.3s 对 170s；三轮修复将 k=2 工作流从 66.7% 提升至 81.2%。SOP-Bench 8 个流程：无修复时 80.6% 低于 few-shot 生成 83.6%，经带标签失败修复后达 87.8%、每任务 0.40s，而 agent 为 85.5%、46.6s。

Conclusion: 将 SOP 编译为代码驱动的工作流可显著降低延迟与成本，性能接近甚至经修复后超过 agent 与 few-shot 基线；LLM 推理说明是提取步骤条件的关键信号，且规则文档的中间表示便于跨平台复用。

Abstract: Business process automation runs the same Standard Operating Procedure (SOP) again and again on new inputs. Large Language Model (LLM) agents can follow an SOP written in natural language, but they call an LLM to choose every step of every run, which adds latency and cost at scale. We present ReTrace, which turns an SOP and a few logged agent runs into a reusable workflow in which code, not an LLM, selects the next step. The agent's reasoning, the explanation it writes with each tool call, often states why it took an action; the tool calls alone do not. ReTrace extracts the condition behind each step, merges these conditions across runs into a compact rule document, and generates an executable workflow from it. Only this last stage depends on the platform, so rule documents can be compiled for different orchestration platforms. On $79$ procedures from our production platform, ReTrace workflows reach $93.8\%$ completion, against $96.8\%$ for the agent, at $24\%$ lower cost per task. On $τ^2$-Bench, they reach $75.8\%$ task success (agent: $80.6\%$) and run in $1.3$s instead of $170$s; three rounds of repair on training failures lift $k{=}2$ workflows from $66.7\%$ to $81.2\%$. On eight SOP-Bench procedures, ReTrace without repair trails few-shot generation ($80.6\%$ vs. $83.6\%$), but repair from labeled training failures lifts it to $87.8\%$ at $0.40$s per task, compared to $85.5\%$ in $46.6$s for the agent.

</details>


### [104] [Comparative Analysis of Agent Evaluation Frameworks: Stability, Detection, and Discovery in Enterprise Analytics Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14493)
*Howard Zhang,Chuck Arvin,Logan Brown,Mihir Bhatia,Neelu Choudhary,Lily Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段对比研究，比较三种LLM分析型智能体评估范式（基于轨迹的LLM评判、纯文本LLM评判+红队测试、确定性启发式），在冻结执行轨迹上评估两个分析智能体，发现基于轨迹的框架综合性能最佳，并建议采用“轨迹评估+对抗红队”的双框架策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中部署基于LLM的分析型智能体需要能可靠检测复杂多工具工作流失败的评价框架，但现有框架的范式差异与适用边界缺乏系统比较。

Method: 在受控研究环境中使用冻结执行轨迹，对两个分析智能体进行三阶段对比：(1)重复测量量化评估框架稳定性；(2)在72个有真值标注的测试用例上测量已知风险检测能力；(3)评估未知风险发现能力。比较对象为三种范式的框架：基于轨迹的LLM评判、纯文本LLM评判结合红队测试、确定性启发式规则。

Result: 阶段1：97%的配置达到稳定性阈值，且稳定性模式与智能体无关。阶段2：基于轨迹的框架取得最佳精确率-召回率平衡（F1=0.90），纯文本评估召回率相当但误报数高5倍。阶段3：新发现24个未知失败，功能测试与安全红队测试的发现零重叠。

Conclusion: (1)评估框架可获得的信息量决定评估质量；(2)功能评估与安全评估互补；(3)基于LLM的评估框架能捕捉人类评审者遗漏的细微错误。建议采用双框架策略：基于轨迹的评估用于功能评价，对抗性红队用于安全验证。

Abstract: Deploying LLM-based analytics agents in enterprise settings requires evaluation frameworks that can reliably detect failures across complex, multi-tool workflows. We present a three-phase comparative study of three evaluation frameworks, each representing a distinct evaluation paradigm (trace-based LLM judging, text-only LLM judging with red-teaming, and deterministic heuristics), applied to two analytics agents in a controlled research setting using frozen execution traces. Phase 1 quantifies evaluation harness stability through repeated measurements, finding that 97% of configurations meet the stability threshold and that stability patterns are agent-independent. Phase 2 measures known risk detection on 72 ground-truth test cases, where the trace-based framework achieves the best precision-recall balance (F1=0.90) while text-only evaluation matches on recall but generates 5x more false alarms. Phase 3 evaluates unknown risk discovery, uncovering 24 novel failures with zero overlap between functional testing and security red-teaming. Our results demonstrate that (1) the information available to the evaluation harness determines evaluation quality, (2) functional and security evaluation are complementary, and (3) LLM-based evaluation harnesses surface subtle errors that human reviewers miss. We recommend a two-framework strategy: trace-based evaluation for functional assessment and adversarial red-teaming for security validation.

</details>


### [105] [Contrastive Discovery: Open-Ended Scientific Discovery over Competing Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14514)
*Ziang Liu,James J. Kim,Yijia Dai,Jennifer J. Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"对比发现"（Contrastive Discovery），用互斥且穷尽的对比集替代单一假设，使LLM发现智能体能在同一证据下定位被支持的具体解释，而非仅拒绝原假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体采用逐点假设检验（仅检验h与其否定¬h），证据拒绝h时只学到"该解释不太可能"，却无法获知证据支持哪种替代解释，导致发现过程信息利用不充分、问题无法落地解决。

Method: 将单一假设替换为对比集：一个科学问题配以互斥且完备（MECE）的竞争性解释集合。实验以证据在该集合上引起信念重分布的程度打分，当信念集中于某一解释时判定问题解决。由于对比集只改变智能体"提议与打分"的单元，现有智能体可直接复用已收集证据重评估，或用于指导搜索。

Result: 六个领域中，逐点检验下信念偏移最大的候选中89%为拒绝；同一证据下对比集将其中的73%解析到具体替代解释；以对比集指导搜索时，相同实验预算下解决的问题数比逐点惊奇度多67%。盲测中38名人类专家在78%的已解决问题上给出相同解释，在逐点智能体拒绝即停止的问题上一致率达82%。

Conclusion: 显式引入竞争性解释可让发现智能体在同等证据与验证预算下解决更多科学问题，是对比集作为假设空间构造单元的有效性证据。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used for open-ended, data-driven scientific discovery, yet most of them evaluate hypotheses pointwise, testing an empirical claim $h$ only against its complement $\neg h$. When evidence rejects $h$, the agent learns that one explanation is unlikely but not which alternative the evidence supports. To recover that alternative, we introduce Contrastive Discovery, which replaces the single hypothesis with a contrast set, a scientific question paired with mutually exclusive and collectively exhaustive competing explanations. Each experiment is scored by how its evidence redistributes belief across the set, and the question is resolved when belief concentrates on one explanation. Because contrast sets change only the unit an agent proposes and scores, an existing agent can use them to re-evaluate evidence it has already collected or to guide its search. Across six domains, $89\%$ of the pointwise candidates with the largest belief shifts are rejections. On the same evidence, contrast sets resolve $73\%$ of these candidates to a specific alternative. When contrast sets guide search, the agent resolves $67\%$ more questions than pointwise surprisal under the same experiment budget. In a blinded study, $38$ human experts chose the same explanation on $78\%$ of resolved questions and on $82\%$ of questions where the pointwise agent rejected its hypothesis and stopped. Together, these results support explicit competing explanations as a way for discovery agents to resolve more scientific questions from the same evidence and validation budget.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Understanding Computing Instructors' Perceptions of Student Sense of Belonging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13237)
*Ritesh Kanchi,Teresa Luo,Sam Grunebaum,Alex Oesterling*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对美国九位入门计算课程教师的半结构化访谈，揭示教师如何定义、测量和支持学生归属感，以及相应干预的障碍，并提出兼顾教师认知与既有研究的归属干预设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究强调教师对学生归属感的影响，但较少考察计算领域教师自身如何感知、理解并尝试支持学生归属感，这一视角缺口限制了归属干预的设计与实践。

Method: 采用九次半结构化定性访谈，对象为美国大型公立大学入门计算课程教师；围绕归属的定义与测量、所采用的干预措施及其有效性障碍三方面问题进行分析。

Result: 教师通过价值与身份表征、自我效能感和共同体来定义归属；借助问卷、观察与学生表现进行测量；认为归属受先前经验、专业、学生与院校文化及代表性影响；实施面向学生的干预并围绕归属设计课程；同时识别出多层面挑战。

Conclusion: 研究指出设计归属干预应同时参考既有研究与教师的概念化理解，并关注助教使用、学生期望平衡及适应影响归属的社会趋势等张力。

Abstract: Students' sense of belonging has been described as a feeling of community acceptance, respect, inclusion, and membership. In computing, student belonging is shaped by psychological, social, academic, and environmental factors, including their relationships with instructors. However, despite prior work emphasizing instructors' influence on student belonging, limited research has examined how computing instructors perceive, understand, and attempt to support belonging. Through nine semi-structured qualitative interviews with introductory computing instructors at large public universities in the United States, we investigated how instructors define, measure, and conceptualize belonging; what interventions they use to support student belonging; and what barriers limit the efficacy of these interventions. Our findings show that instructors defined belonging through value and identity representation, self-efficacy, and community; relied on surveys, observations, and student performance to measure belonging; saw belonging to be influenced by prior experience, major, student and institutional culture, and representation; implemented student-oriented interventions and designed courses around belonging; and identified multi-level challenges to supporting belonging. These results highlight opportunities to design belonging interventions that attend to both prior work and instructor conceptualizations, while also considering tensions in utilizing teaching assistants, balancing students' expectations, and adapting to broader societal trends affecting belonging.

</details>


### [107] [Developing an Operational Model for an Internet of Data Based Trusted Data Space for Vocational Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13520)
*Yalan Dong,Hao Tian,Hongyang Jia,Chunxue Wang,Wenchang Lei*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对职业教育数据流通中权属不清、信任缺失与激励不足的问题，基于"数据互联网"构建可信数据空间，并设计多主体运营模型以支撑区域职业教育数据生态的可持续运营。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业教育数据流通长期受制于三方面障碍：数据权属界定不清、跨域流通缺乏信任基础、参与方激励不足，因此需要新的底层技术范式与运营机制来支撑可信的跨域数据流通。

Method: 以"数据互联网"（Internet of Data）为底层技术支撑，构建面向职业教育的可信数据空间；在其上设计多主体运营模型，包含价值网络、产品组合、收益分配与治理规则四个模块，并强调数据主权保护与激励相容。

Result: 摘要未给出实证数据或量化结果；主要产出为一个覆盖价值网络、产品组合、收益分配与治理规则的多主体运营模型框架。

Conclusion: 该模型为区域职业教育数据生态的可持续运营提供了参考方案，其核心设计原则为数据主权保护与激励相容。

Abstract: Data circulation in vocational education has long been constrained by unclear ownership, a lack of trust, and insufficient incentives. The Internet of Data provides a new underlying technology for enabling trusted cross-domain data circulation. Building on an Internet of Data based trusted data space for vocational education, this paper develops a multi-stakeholder operational model encompassing a value network, a product portfolio, revenue allocation, and governance rules. Emphasizing data sovereignty protection and incentive compatibility, the model provides a reference for the sustainable operation of regional vocational education data ecosystems.

</details>


### [108] [Repertoires of Contention: Building computational tools and infrastructures for community resistance to bitcoin mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13527)
*Marina Johnson-Zafiris,Owen Marshall,Jen Liu,Joseph Ferdinando,Steven J. Jackson,Chris Csíkszentmihályi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文通过多种计算技术与社区共同参与，围绕纽约州北部Greenidge比特币矿场发电厂的社会环境影响的争议，探索以计算手段支持社区抗争话语与行动的可能性，主张HCI应走向更具政治参与性的研究取向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI及相关领域已有将社区成员作为共同设计者、协作者与决策者以挑战结构性权力壁垒与知识鸿沟的研究传统；本文延续这一脉络，针对Greenidge比特币挖矿发电厂带来的社会环境问题及监管惰性、热污染等现实争议，试图弥补社区在知识生产与权力博弈中的不对等地位。

Method: 采用协作式社区参与方法，与社区团体进行持续互动，运用多样化的计算技术（computational techniques）对矿场设施的环境影响与监管问题进行数据化、可视化与知识生产，作为支撑社区行动的技术干预手段。

Result: 通过协作实践，产生了新的、具有批判性的环境知识，并形成了"计算支持下的抗争剧目"（computationally-supported repertoires of contention），即用以质疑并挑战塑造环境争议结果的知识—权力不平等关系的手段集合。

Conclusion: 此类社区计算参与能够支持更具政治参与性的HCI形态，通过战略性的社会技术干预与联盟，致力于纠正和修复财富、知识与影响力方面的不平等。

Abstract: Recent work in human-computer interaction and allied fields has sought to engage community members as co-designers, facilitators, and decision makers to challenge structural power barriers and knowledge gaps. In this paper, we apply a diverse set of computational techniques to engage and address community concerns surrounding the socio-environmental impacts of the Greenidge Bitcoin mining power plant facility in upstate New York. Through collaborative interactions with community groups, addressing problems ranging from regulatory inertia to thermal water pollution, we aim to facilitate not just the production of new and critical environmental knowledge but also new computationally-supported repertoires of contention: means of contesting the unequal relations of knowledge and power that structure and often determine the outcomes of environmental controversies. We argue such engagements can help support more politically engaged forms of HCI, oriented towards efforts to redress and repair disparities in wealth, knowledge, and influence through strategic sociotechnical interventions and alliances.

</details>


### [109] [Perspicacious: An Educational Intelligence Platform for Instructional Designers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13549)
*Yumou Wei,Michael C Sutton,Paulo F. Carvalho*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出并评估一体化教学智能平台"Perspicacious"，将题目生成与知识组件(KC)发现整合于单一界面，弥合AI教育研究到实践的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学设计者需为明确界定的知识组件(KC)编写测评题目，此过程耗时且高度依赖专家人力。现有自动题目生成与KC发现方法多停留于研究原型，缺乏可供设计者直接使用的易用工具，形成研究到实践的鸿沟。

Method: 开发名为Perspicacious的教育智能平台，在统一界面中整合题目生成与KC发现；基于17次生产运行校准成本模型；开展8名教学设计者参与的形成性、部分"绿野仙踪"(Wizard-of-Oz)研究，采用SUS量表评估可用性并收集需求。

Result: 成本模型揭示成本-时间权衡：从576题题库中抽取KC，花费US$1.98–4.11、耗时36–158分钟，取决于预算。可用性感知良好（SUS均值78.8），并识别出可作为部署前提的具体需求。

Conclusion: 统一式平台可操作化AI教育研究成果，支持教学设计实践；但用户研究揭示的具体需求应视为实际部署的前置条件，未来需据此迭代完善。

Abstract: Designing effective instruction requires instructional designers to create assessment questions aligned with clearly defined knowledge components (KCs). These KCs represent specific knowledge or skills that students must acquire. This process is often time-consuming and relies heavily on expert labor. Although AI for education research has produced a growing body of automated question-generation and KC-discovery methods, few have moved beyond research prototypes into user-friendly tools that instructional designers can readily use. To bridge the research-to-practice gap, we developed and evaluated an educational intelligence platform that unifies question generation and KC discovery behind a single interface to support instructional content development. Named "Perspicacious," our platform aims to help instructional designers "see through" complex data and algorithms to derive instructional insights. A cost model calibrated on 17 production runs revealed a cost-time trade-off: extracting KCs from a 576-question bank cost US $1.98-4.11 and took 36-158 minutes to complete, depending on the budget. A formative, partially Wizard-of-Oz study with eight instructional designers found strong perceived usability (mean SUS 78.8) and surfaced concrete requirements that we treat as prerequisites for deployment.

</details>


### [110] [Transparency Disclosures for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13873)
*Jacob Davies,Theo Bearman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出前沿AI开发者应公开五项指标以追踪思维链(CoT)可监控性所受威胁，并审计Anthropic、OpenAI与Google DeepMind的现状：无一家覆盖全部指标，但每项指标均已被至少一家披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控是监控先进AI系统最有价值的工具之一，但正受到威胁；当前缺乏可追踪这些威胁、便于协调以维护CoT可监控性的统一披露框架。

Method: 基于CoT监控所依赖的五步链条，识别出五个高度相关且披露可行的指标（第三方CoT访问与基础架构规格、CoT自然性、CoT忠实性、检测有效性、性能提升/增益），并对Anthropic、OpenAI和Google DeepMind的现行披露进行审计。

Result: 审计显示没有开发者覆盖完整指标集，但每项指标都至少被一家前沿开发者报告过；现有披露主要依赖开发者自评。

Conclusion: 建议开展具备比公开披露更深访问权限的跨开发者审计，可由嵌入开发者的评估人员或独立验证组织(IVO)实施。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is one of the most valuable tools for monitoring advanced AI systems, but it is under threat. We argue that frontier AI developers should report specific indicators to make these threats trackable and to better facilitate coordination to preserve CoT monitorability. We identify five indicators that are both highly relevant and reasonably feasible to disclose, one for each link in a five-step chain on which CoT monitoring depends. The first, third-party CoT access and base architecture specifications, also underpins external verification of the rest. The others span CoT quality (naturalness and faithfulness) and monitoring effectiveness (detection and uplift). An audit of current disclosures by Anthropic, OpenAI, and Google DeepMind shows that no developer covers the full set, but that every indicator has been reported by at least one frontier developer. Because current disclosures rely largely on developers' own evaluations, we also recommend that a cross-developer audit be repeated with deeper access than public disclosures allow, either by embedded evaluators working within developers or by an Independent Verification Organization.

</details>


### [111] [Advancing a Global Framework for AI Safety and Governance for the Well-being of Humanity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13968)
*Zeng Yi,Seán Ó hÉigeartaigh,Wang Zhengqi,Lu Enmeng,Chen Yu,Cao Gongce,Guo Xiaoyang,Kang Yanrong,Han Kaiyu,Fan Jinyu,Xie Jiawei,Han Zhengqiang,Wang Jin,Huangfu Cunqing,Bao Aorigele,Dame Wendy Hall,Lin Lin,Duan Weiwen,Wang Rong,Zhang Junlin,Tang Xinhua,Vincent C. Müller,Chen Jingjing,Li Na,Cheng Kai,Sebastian Sunday Grève,Danil Kerimi,Bernd Holznagel,Anna Abramova,Jimena Sofia Viveros Alvarez,Edson Prestes,Shen Juncheng,Liu Yongmou,Zhang Jiji,Nada Laabidi,Yao Xin,Zhou Kai,George Chen,Fu Chunhui,Liu Xiaochun,Guo Sumin,Hu Naying,Qiao Qian,Meng Wei,Zhang Linghan,Tan Zhixing,Helen Meng,Qiao Yu,Bu Yuyan,Fan Wei,Wang Xingguang,Wang Feng,Gong Xinqi,Xia Wenhui,Wang Mengyin,Tao Feng,Hu Yongqi,Charuka Senal Damunupola,Cheng Ming,Liu Weichen,Nirosha Ananda,Peng Tao,Tao Tao,Wang Fengqiong,Wang Jianbing,Wang Tong,Wang Wei,Wang Xin,Wu Shuyan,Yang Yaodong,Yang Zhongliang,John Yeoh,Zhang Rong,Zhou Yuan,Zhang Xueli,Liang Hao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 世界互联网大会人工智能专委会2025年报告系统梳理全球AI安全与治理实践，识别关键议题，借鉴多边治理经验，围绕机制设计提出以联合国为中心的全球AI安全与治理框架及政策建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球AI安全与治理探索分散，存在亟待解决的关键问题，缺乏统合性的全球治理框架，需借助多边主义与人类命运共同体理念推动AI造福全人类。

Method: 系统性综述全球探索与实践，识别关键议题，借鉴其他领域多边治理经验，以机制设计为核心，从安全、包容、责任、协调、权威高效五个维度构建治理体系。

Result: 提出建立以联合国为中心的全球AI安全与治理框架的制度安排与政策建议，面向政府、国际组织、企业与研究机构提供参考。

Conclusion: 报告为推进以人为本、向上向善的全球AI治理提供思路与动力，确保AI技术真正服务于全人类福祉。

Abstract: The Report "Advancing a Global Framework for AI Safety and Governance for the Well-being of Humanity" is an important outcome of the AI Safety and Governance Program of World Internet Conference Specialized Committee on Artificial Intelligence in 2025. This report provides a systematic review of current global explorations and practices in AI safety and governance, identifies key issues that need to be addressed, and draws upon multilateral governance experiences from other domains. Centered on mechanism design, it explores how to build a global AI safety and governance system that ensures safety and security, promotes inclusiveness, clarifies responsibilities and accountabilities, strengthens effective coordination, and ensures authoritative and efficient governance. Guided by the principles of multilateralism and the vision of building a community with a shared future for humankind, the report proposes institutional measures and policy recommendations to establish a United Nations-centered global AI safety and governance framework. It aims to serve as a reference for governments, international organizations, enterprises, and research institutions. The report is to contribute insights and momentum to advancing people-centered and beneficial AI governance worldwide, ensuring that AI technologies truly serve the well-being of all humanity.

</details>


### [112] [Can AI be a 'Researcher Without Borders'? Auditing Bias in AI-Assisted Social Science Research about China and the US](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14288)
*Yuan Zhang,Thales Bertaglia,Ralph Schroeder*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文构建基准任务，考察美、中、欧开发的LLM在社会科学问答中是否存在"国家偏向"，结果整体无政治偏见但引用来源存在差异，部分跨国比较题仍偏向特定国家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在算法与数学任务上表现优异，但其作为社会科学研究助手的适用性存疑：社科问题常缺乏公认可量化的标准答案。研究者与学生日益依赖AI生成研究构想，若模型偏向其开发国或该国学术传统所关联的社会理论，将扭曲研究发现、损害科学可信度并误导公众。

Method: 设计基准任务，要求模型就涉及美国与中国及更广泛社科问题的题目撰写短文。操纵三个条件：问题类型（跨国比较、国别特定、一般问题）、模型（2个美国开发、2个中国开发、1个欧洲开发）、提示方式（基础写作请求、研究者身份、美国研究者身份、中国研究者身份）。由两名具备中美社科背景的研究者人工标注偏见与来源使用情况。

Result: 总体上，美国、中国及欧洲模型均未表现出政治偏见，但所引用来源存在差异；部分比较类问题仍偏向某一国家，且这种倾向在特定问题上更为明显。

Conclusion: 模型存在来源选择差异与问题特定的国别偏向，研究者与学生在使用AI开展社会科学研究时应保持谨慎。

Abstract: Although large language models have demonstrated strong performance on algorithmic and mathematical tasks, their suitability as social science research assistants remains unclear, particularly because many social science questions lack agreed-upon, quantifiable benchmark answers. Evaluating this suitability is important since researchers and students increasingly rely on AI to generate research ideas. When questions concern the countries in which models were developed, bias may be exacerbated: Do AI models favour the countries where they were developed or the social theories associated with those countries' scholarly traditions? Such biases could distort research findings, undermine scientific credibility, and distort findings when they are disseminated to the public. To investigate whether models exhibit country-focused bias in social science research, we designed a benchmarking task requiring AI to write short essays on social science questions in relation to the US and China as well as some broader social science questions. We varied three conditions: question type (cross-country comparisons, country-specific questions, and general questions); model (two US-developed, two China-developed, and one Europe-developed model); and prompting modality (a base essay-writing request, a researcher identity, a US researcher identity, and a Chinese researcher identity). Responses were manually annotated for bias and source use by two researchers with experience in social science in relation to China and the US. Our analyses show that, on the whole, US and Chinese models and the European one are not politically biased but differ in the sources they cite. Still, some comparative questions favour one country over another, and this tendency is more pronounced for certain questions. These findings suggest that researchers and students should exercise caution when using AI for social science research.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [DocFS: File Systems as Agentic Reasoning Environments for Complex Multimodal Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12957)
*Bingdian Zhang,Zixuan Li,Long Bai,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 DocFS，将原始多模态文档重组为文件系统，用目录映射章节层级、显式链接连接文本与图表公式，并借助导航/搜索/阅读等文件操作工具让智能体迭代探索，在 MMLongBench-Doc 与 FinRAGBench-V 上取得 SOTA，且推理更快更省 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 VLM 的智能体依赖环境如何暴露信息和提供操作工具。面向复杂多模态文档问答构建有效环境仍困难：文档跨多页、多模态内容分布于层级章节并由跨模态关联连接；现有方法只把文档组织为结构化表示，仅处理环境的信息面，缺少与结构对齐的工具，限制了智能体充分利用结构的能力。

Method: 观察到文件系统天然将结构组织与对齐工具结合（目录/文件编码文档结构，文件操作提供内容访问工具），据此提出 DocFS：将原始文档重组为文件系统，目录镜像章节层级，显式链接连接文本与图、表、公式；基于导航、搜索、阅读等成熟文件系统工具，使智能体迭代探索文档结构、定位相关证据并理解细粒度局部信息。

Result: 配合 Qwen3.5-Plus，在 MMLongBench-Doc 和 FinRAGBench-V 上达到 SOTA，分别比最强基线高出 2.9 和 4.3 个百分点；推理阶段速度最高提升 10.3 倍，token 用量最多减少 10.1 倍。

Conclusion: 文件系统范式可同时统一文档的结构表示与配套操作工具，使智能体在长文档多模态问答中兼具准确性、效率与可解释的探索路径。

Abstract: VLM-based agents solve complex tasks by iteratively interacting with environments, and their effectiveness depends heavily on how the environment exposes information and provides tools for acting on it. However, constructing an effective environment for complex multimodal document question answering remains challenging: documents usually span many pages, with multimodal contents distributed across hierarchical sections and linked by cross-modal associations, while existing methods organize documents into structured representations that address only the information side of the environment and lack tools aligned with the structure, limiting agents' ability to fully exploit it. We observe that file systems naturally combine structure organization with aligned tools: directories and files encode document structure, while file operations provide rich tools to access file contents. Building on this insight, we propose DocFS, a file-system-based agentic framework that reorganizes raw documents as file systems, where directories mirror the section hierarchy and explicit links connect text with figures, tables, and equations. Based on well-established file system tools such as navigation, search, and reading, agents can iteratively explore the document structure, locate relevant evidence, and understand fine-grained local information. Paired with Qwen3.5-Plus, DocFS achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMLongBench-Doc and FinRAGBench-V, outperforming the strongest baselines by 2.9 and 4.3 percentage points, respectively, and is up to 10.3x faster with up to 10.1x fewer tokens than the strongest baseline during reasoning.

</details>


### [114] [Exploiting Feature-invariant Scaling Laws for Efficient A/B Experimentation in Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13539)
*Zhiliang Chen,Sammy Nammari,Jiahao Su,Xiaoqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文发现现代推荐模型具有"特征不变的缩放律"(feature-invariant scaling)，并据此提出一种结合统计推断的小规模实验方法，用置信区间预测A/B实验中特征改变在全规模下的效果，从而以极低算力筛除无效或退化的特征改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中每次特征改动都做全规模A/B实验算力开销过大，而单一小规模实验结果又常常具有误导性，缺少可靠、低成本的决策手段。

Method: 提出特征不变缩放律假设：不同特征改动下模型性能变化仍遵循可预测的缩放规律；基于该规律用统计推断在小规模实验中预测全规模效果并给出置信区间，用于过滤中性或退化的特征改动。在Wukong、HSTU和基于LLM的推荐模型三类模型族、40余种架构/优化/数据改动上验证。

Result: 方法能准确区分有益、中性与退化的特征改动，且仅需全规模评估的一小部分算力；还发现了若干可将HSTU在MovieLens-20M上SOTA性能进一步提升的改动。

Conclusion: 缩放律可被实际利用来支撑工业级推荐系统的算力高效决策，用小规模实验替代全规模A/B测试进行特征筛选具有可行性。

Abstract: Researchers and engineers improve recommendation systems by performing A/B experiments with new model architectures, optimization methods, and data features. However, running full-scale experiments (e.g., full-model training with online data) for every feature change is prohibitively computationally intensive, while the result from a single small-scale experiment is often misleading. To address this challenge, we show that modern recommendation models exhibit feature-invariant scaling: the change of a model's performance follows a predictable scaling-law even when different feature changes are applied to the baseline model. We introduce an efficient method that exploits scaling-laws and uses statistical inference to predict (with confidence intervals) the full-scale effect of an A/B experiment in recommendation systems. This enables practitioners to efficiently filter out neutral or regressive feature changes with only small-scale experimentation. We validate our method on three distinct recommendation model families---Wukong, HSTU, and LLM-based recommendation models---across more than 40 architectural, optimization, and data changes. Our method accurately distinguishes beneficial feature changes from neutral and regressive ones while using only a fraction of the compute required for full-scale evaluation. It also identifies several changes that improve HSTU's state-of-the-art performance on MovieLens-20M. Our results demonstrate that scaling laws can be practically exploited to make compute-efficient decisions for improving industrial-scale recommendation systems.

</details>


### [115] [Auditing LLM Pseudo-Label Distillation for Review Informativeness at E-Commerce Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13797)
*M. Emin Dogan,Cansu Er,Aysenur Kulunk,M. Furkan Eseoglu,H. Bahadir Sahin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究在534万条土耳其语电商评论上将Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite作为教师做硬标签蒸馏至紧凑编码器；发现H3类别前缀对教师保真度与编辑一致性的影响方向相反，且学生流水线在单张L4上比教师快50.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低大模型推理成本、实现可部署的评论处理流水线；同时探讨仅以教师保真度作为蒸馏质量指标是否充分，以及上下文前缀（H3类别）对蒸馏效果的影响。

Method: 数据为534万条土耳其电商评论；以Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite为教师生成硬标签，蒸馏至紧凑编码器。评估分两路：(1) 严格的"新SKU"时间切分测试集衡量教师保真度；(2) 独立的、对教师标签盲的审计集（16,000条按时间排序、由一名公司标注员标注）衡量编辑一致性。对比H3类别前缀的有无，并比较CORN与交叉熵损失下的配对差异。

Result: H3类别前缀在全量新SKU测试集上小幅提升教师保真度；但在16,000条审计子集上，反而降低与Gemini及标注员的一致性，且CORN的配对差异比交叉熵更明显。离线实测配置下，单张L4的学生流水线比教师流水线快50.2倍。

Conclusion: 上下文（前缀）效应依赖评估人群，教师保真度本身不足以证明编辑对齐；蒸馏评估需引入对教师盲的人工审计。蒸馏在效率上收益显著（50.2×加速），但质量结论因评估集而异，需谨慎外推。

Abstract: We study hard-label distillation from Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite to compact encoders on 5.34 million Turkish marketplace reviews. A strict new-SKU temporal test measures teacher fidelity; a separate, teacher-label-blind audit by one company annotator measures editorial agreement. An H3 category prefix slightly improves teacher fidelity on the full new-SKU test. On the 16,000-review chronological audit subset, it instead reduces agreement with both Gemini and the annotator, with a clearer paired difference for CORN than for cross entropy. Thus the context effect depends on the evaluation population, and teacher fidelity alone does not establish editorial alignment. Under the measured offline configurations, a single-L4 student pipeline processes reviews 50.2 times faster than the teacher pipeline.

</details>


### [116] [Controlled Knowledge Engineering for Taxonomy Induction with Tool-Grounded LLM Agents and Runtime-Adaptive Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13966)
*Jonas Limniatis,Bennet Schaarmann,Georg Reuber,Robert Stahlbock*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TIGA——将分类体系构建建模为基于显式状态与受限动作空间的迭代决策过程，结合可执行编辑工具、交互记忆与运行时自适应组织策略，在四个基准上多数配置取得最优结构质量，但代价是更高的token、调用量与推理时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从固定候选实体集自动构建taxonomy需保证层级完整、结构有效与语义一致，而LLM自由形式的迭代生成易出现实体遗漏或幻觉、非法层级关系及组织原则不一致等问题。

Method: TIGA（Taxonomy Induction with Tool-Grounded Agents）：(1) 可执行的taxonomy编辑工具；(2) 记录工具调用、理由与结果的交互记忆；(3) 推理过程中动态修订的运行时自适应组织策略；(4) 可选全局审阅器，依据候选集与当前策略评估已构建分类体系。实验在4个基准taxonomy、2种LLM骨干上与单次与迭代提示基线对比，并进行消融。

Result: 在多数评测配置下结构质量最优，但并非在所有精度指标上全面占优；收益伴随显著更高的token消耗、API调用量与推理时间。消融显示策略与交互记忆组件收益稳定，全局审阅的效果依赖数据集与可用修订预算。

Conclusion: 工具接地与运行时策略自适应是提升结构化知识受控构建的有前景但成本较高的机制。

Abstract: Taxonomies are structured knowledge representations that support semantic search, recommendation, product catalog organization, and retrieval-augmented generation. Their automated construction from a fixed set of candidate entities requires more than plausible text generation: the resulting hierarchy must be complete, structurally valid, and semantically coherent. Large Language Models (LLMs) are promising for this task, but iterative free-form generation remains vulnerable to omitted or hallucinated entities, invalid hierarchical relations, and inconsistent organizational principles. We introduce TIGA (Taxonomy Induction with Tool-Grounded Agents), a knowledge-engineering architecture that formulates taxonomy induction as an iterative decision process over an explicit state and constrained action space. TIGA combines executable taxonomy-editing tools, an memory that records tool calls, rationales, and outcomes, and a runtime-adaptive organizational policy that the agent revises during inference. An optional global reviewer evaluates the constructed taxonomy against the candidate set and the active policy. We evaluate TIGA on four benchmark taxonomies using two LLM backbones and compare it with single-shot and iterative prompting-based baselines. TIGA achieves the strongest structural-quality results in most evaluated configurations, but does not dominate on every precision measure. The gains are accompanied by substantially higher token consumption, API-call volume, and inference time. Ablation results show consistent benefits from the policy and interaction-memory components, whereas the effect of global review depends on the dataset and the available revision budget. These findings characterize tool grounding and runtime policy adaptation as promising but costly mechanisms for improving the controlled construction of structured knowledge representations.

</details>


### [117] [Data Quality Challenges in Multimodal Tourism Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13972)
*Zehui Wang,Wolfram Höpken,Dietmar Jannach*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 现有推荐系统日益采用多模态数据提升性能，但其底层数据质量关注不足。本文以旅游推荐为场景，基于Yelp与Instagram真实数据剖析四类数据质量问题，并提出质量感知融合、受控LLM内容生成与用户分群三个未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统广泛引入社交媒体等多模态数据以提升建模效果，但数据本身的多模态质量问题（噪声、幻觉、模态冲突、人口统计偏差）被忽视，可能削弱模型鲁棒性与可靠性，因此有必要系统审视真实场景中的数据质量挑战。

Method: 立场/展望型研究：以旅游推荐为具体情境，对来自Yelp与Instagram的真实数据集进行经验性考察，归纳并分类实际遇到的数据质量问题，即虚假评论噪声、LLM生成内容的幻觉、模态间矛盾以及人口统计偏差；在此基础上提出若干未来研究方向。

Result: 识别出四类典型数据质量问题：虚假评论带来的文本噪声、LLM生成内容的幻觉、跨模态信息矛盾、以及人口统计偏差；并据此提出三条改进路径——质量感知的多模态融合、结构化且受控的LLM内容生成以抑制幻觉、以及用户群体差异化建模。

Conclusion: 多模态推荐研究的瓶颈已从模型设计部分转移至数据质量治理；通过质量感知融合、可控LLM生成与用户分群策略，可提升多模态推荐系统的鲁棒性与可靠性。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems increasingly consider diverse data modalities to enhance model performance. However, the quality of the underlying multimodal data (e.g. data from social media platforms) has received limited attention so far. Focusing on tourism recommendation, we examine real-world challenges encountered when using datasets from Yelp and Instagram. Issues include noise from fake reviews, hallucinated content from LLMs, modality contradictions, and demographic bias. We outline future directions such as quality-aware fusion, structured and controlled LLM-based content generation to reduce hallucinations, and user group differentiation to improve the robustness and reliability of multimodal recommendation systems.

</details>


### [118] [LUMIR: Decoupled Multimodal Representation Learning from Heterogeneous Data for Next-POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14004)
*Zehui Wang,Wolfram Höpken,Dietmar Jannach*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出解耦式多模态表示学习框架LUMIR，基于对比学习，在多模态POI下一个兴趣点推荐任务上优于现有融合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源异构旅游多模态数据日益丰富，有效表示学习对提升推荐性能很重要；但现有方法将多模态融合与特定下游模型的架构和优化目标紧耦合，限制了所学表示的通用性与可复用性。

Method: LUMIR为基于对比学习的解耦多模态表示学习框架：先用大语言模型（LLM）将异构数据源的多模态信息语义统一到共享文本表示空间；再设计交互感知融合机制建模跨模态交互；并以序列信号作为结构约束指导表示学习。

Result: 在多模态POI数据集的下一个POI推荐设定下，跨多个序列推荐骨干网络进行评测，LUMIR在准确性指标与超越准确性（beyond-accuracy）指标上均持续优于现有多模态融合基线。

Conclusion: 将表示学习与下游优化目标解耦，是学习可复用多模态表示的有效范式；代码已开源。

Abstract: As multimodal tourism-related information collected from heterogeneous data sources has become increasingly available, learning effective representations has attracted attention for improving recommendation performance. Existing approaches often tightly couple multimodal fusion with the architecture and optimization objective of a specific downstream model, which limits the generality and reusability of the learned representations. To address these limitations, we propose LUMIR, a decoupled multimodal representation learning framework based on contrastive learning. Multimodal information collected from heterogeneous data sources is first semantically unified into a shared textual representation space using large language models (LLMs). An interaction-aware fusion mechanism is then designed to model cross-modal interactions, while sequential signals are incorporated as a structural constraint to guide representation learning. To evaluate the learned representations, experiments are conducted on a multimodal point-of-interest (POI) dataset under the next-POI recommendation setting across multiple sequential recommendation backbones. The results show that LUMIR consistently outperforms existing multimodal fusion baselines in both accuracy-oriented and beyond-accuracy metrics, demonstrating that decoupling representation learning from downstream optimization provides an effective paradigm for learning reusable multimodal representations. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzehui/LUMIR.

</details>


### [119] [From Show Programs to Linked Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14037)
*Clarisse Bardiot,Bernard Jacquemin,Jacob Hart,George Bruseker,Antonios Lagarias,Pierre-Carl Langlais,Brandon Farnsworth,Jeanne Fras*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出结合视觉语言模型（VLM）、Linked Art本体扩展与知识图谱约束的语义标注工作流，将阿维尼翁戏剧节演出节目单批量转化为结构化、可互操作的关联数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗产机构收藏的演出节目单数量庞大、难以逐件编目、基本未数字化，导致可及性差；表演艺术研究缺乏如视觉艺术"作品全集目录"（catalogue raisonné）式的系统化史料基础。

Method: 以法国国家图书馆藏阿维尼翁戏剧节节目单（1947年至今）为案例：用视觉语言模型对异质遗产文档图像做文本转录；针对表演艺术定制扩展Linked Art本体；并比较多种自动语义标注方法，其中通过知识图谱约束大语言模型生成以抑制幻觉。

Result: VLM文本转录准确率超过98%；知识图谱约束显著降低语义标注中的幻觉。工作流产出结构化、可互操作的数据。

Conclusion: 该工作流为大规模、可互操作的表演艺术史学研究开辟新路径，包括构建表演艺术家作品全集目录这一在视觉艺术中常见、而表演艺术研究尚缺的学术形式。

Abstract: Many heritage institutions hold extensive collections of show programs. Too numerous to be cataloged individually, they remain difficult to access and are still largely undigitized. This paper presents a workflow for transforming such documents into structured, interoperable data by combining vision-language models (VLMs), a custom extension of the Linked Art ontology for the performing arts, and different approaches for automatic semantic data annotation. Using the Festival d'Avignon programs (1947--present), preserved at the Biblioth{è}que nationale de France, as a case study, we demonstrate how VLMs achieve more than 98% text transcription accuracy from heterogeneous heritage document images and how constraining large language model generation through a knowledge graph significantly reduces hallucinations in semantic annotation. We argue that this workflow opens new possibilities for large-scale, interoperable performing arts historiography, including the creation of catalogues raisonn{é}s for performing artists, a form of scholarship common in visual arts but absent in performing arts studies.

</details>


### [120] [Ontology-Guided Knowledge Graph Construction and Robust Evidence Retrieval for Indian Knowledge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14050)
*Anand Kaniyaar Sriranga Prasad,Avinash Holenarasipur Nagendra,Mohan Raghavan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文提出本体引导的知识图谱（KG）流水线，将Qwen3-32B抽取的类型化实体与关系链接回原文证据，并与单向量检索（SV）、实体笔记（Entity Notes）的不同组合在Dhara Bench 320题上做描述性对比；结论是按"早期相关性—证据完整性—检索成本"的平衡来选择KG或其并集方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从印度知识体系中检索证据面临三类困难：转写变体（transliteration variants）、名称歧义（ambiguous names）、以及关系分散在多个段落中。传统单向量检索难以应对这些跨段、歧义和拼写变体问题，因此需要能显式建模类型化实体—关系并回溯原文证据的检索方案。

Method: 1) 本体引导的KG流水线：用Qwen3-32B抽取类型化实体与关系，并链接至原始文本证据；2) 检索端融合五类机制：sense-aware grounding、有界图遍历（bounded graph traversal）、词法锚点（lexical anchors）、关系向量检索（relationship-vector search）、段落重排（passage reranking）；3) 评测设置：Dhara Bench 320题，对比单独KG、单向量检索SV、以及KG+SV、KG+SV+Entity Notes并集；在BGE与Qwen两种重排器下对齐每题候选数；另有部署时延研究。

Result: 单一KG在两种重排器下均取得最高Precision@5；Qwen下Multi-hop MRR@20为0.8722（完全重排的KG+SV并集为0.7622），并在Counting、Composite、Multi-hop上MRR@20居内部配置之首。KG+SV+Entity Notes并集总Recall@20最高（0.5134 vs KG的0.4347）。15道Multi-hop题上，Qwen KG的完整证据MRR@20为0.4651（SV为0.3372）；两种完整并集在20条结果内完整命中10题，KG为9题。Gemini 3.7 Flash（经人工审核的源映射）在整体MRR与nDCG上更强，内部系统有类别特异性优势；所有比较均为描述性。部署时延：KG平均20.374 s，SV为0.646 s。

Conclusion: KG在早期排序精度与多跳场景的精确性、以及完整证据定位上优于单向量检索，而并集方案在召回/证据完整性上更优；代价是显著更高的检索时延。因此不存在单一最优配置，应按"早期相关性、证据完整性、检索成本"三者的权衡选择KG或其并集，且结论限于描述性范围。

Abstract: Retrieving evidence from Indian Knowledge Systems is complicated by transliteration variants, ambiguous names, and relations distributed across passages. We present an ontology-guided knowledge graph (KG) pipeline that links typed entities and relations extracted with Qwen3-32B to original textual evidence. Retrieval combines sense-aware grounding, bounded graph traversal, lexical anchors, relationship-vector search, and passage reranking. We evaluate KG alone, single-vector retrieval (SV), and KG unions with SV and Entity Notes on 320 questions from Dhara Bench, matching the per-question candidate count across internal systems under BGE and Qwen rerankers. Standalone KG has the highest Precision@5 under both rerankers and the highest MRR@20 in Counting, Composite, and Multi-hop among the evaluated internal configurations. With Qwen, KG achieves Multi-hop MRR@20 of 0.8722, compared with 0.7622 for the fully reranked KG + SV union. The KG + SV + Entity Notes union instead provides the highest overall Recall@20 (0.5134), compared with 0.4347 for KG. On the 15 Multi-hop questions, Qwen KG achieves complete-evidence MRR@20 of 0.4651 versus 0.3372 for SV, while both full unions complete ten questions within twenty results compared with nine for KG. Gemini 3.7 Flash, evaluated through manually reviewed source mappings, re mains stronger on overall MRR and nDCG, while internal systems have category-specific advantages. All comparisons are descriptive. A separate deployment timing study measures mean retrieval times of 20.374 s for KG and 0.646 s for SV. The findings support selecting KG or its unions according to the required balance of early relevance, evidence completeness, and retrieval cost.

</details>


### [121] [From Search to Decision: How Post-Training Shapes Information-Seeking Capabilities in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14149)
*Haohang Huang,Xuan Lu,Mingjin Kuai,Yuxuan Zhang,Wei Ji,Haiyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文围绕大语言模型系统的"信息寻求"能力做系统性综述，提出六项相互关联的能力框架，并按学习信号、优化方法、更新位置与信用分配机制分类梳理，总结针对性行为、端到端表现、搜索控制与泛化方面的实证进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的信息寻求涉及"何时获取信息、到哪里检索、如何评估与使用证据"等决策；近期后训练方法虽改善了部分行为，但目标函数与评测分散在不同任务与系统设定下，缺乏统一视角，因此需要整合。

Method: 综述/框架构建方法：将研究组织为六项相互关联的信息寻求能力，并从四个维度（学习信号、优化方法、更新位置/参数更新位点、信用分配机制）对已有工作进行归类与对比分析。

Result: 现有研究报告了在检索路由、查询改写、多工具探索、证据支撑推理与资源感知搜索等方面的提升，但不同能力的行为证据范围不一致；同时梳理了针对性行为、端到端性能、搜索控制与泛化四类实证进展。

Conclusion: 提出开放挑战：估计前瞻性检索效用、协调多项信息寻求决策、适应陌生检索环境；并讨论用于解读未来进展的评测实践。

Abstract: Information seeking in large language model (LLM) systems increasingly involves decisions about when to acquire information, where to search, and how to evaluate and use evidence. Recent post-training methods have improved several such behaviors, but their objectives and evaluations span different tasks and system settings. This paper organizes research around six interrelated information-seeking capabilities and examines the learning signals, optimization methods, update loci, and credit-assignment mechanisms used to develop them. We synthesize empirical progress in targeted behaviors, end-to-end performance, search control, and generalization. Existing studies report gains in retrieval routing, query refinement, multi-tool exploration, evidence-grounded reasoning, and resource-aware search, although the scope of behavioral evidence varies across capabilities. We identify open challenges in estimating prospective retrieval utility, coordinating information-seeking decisions, and adapting to unfamiliar retrieval environments, and discuss evaluation practices for interpreting future progress.

</details>


### [122] [SHARP: Semantic Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for Next POI Prediction with Dynamic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14220)
*Yuhang Zhan,Silin Zhou,Zhuo Chen,Haoxiang Lei,Yunren Feng,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 SHARP，一种分层智能体框架，通过类别级推理引导具体 POI 预测。它包含维护动态轨迹记忆库的轨迹记忆智能体、多粒度时间感知类别转移图，以及结合二者预测下一类别的类别预测智能体，并以 RQ-VAE 学习语义 ID 与类别引导的 POI 预测器端到端联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 语义推理与图结构的 POI 预测存在三个问题：LLM 语义推理与具体 POI 预测之间难以衔接；用户移动模式随时间演化，需在有限的 LLM 上下文内保留并更新历史知识；全局转移图会平滑掉时间特定的类别转移模式。

Method: 提出 SHARP 分层智能体框架：(1) 轨迹记忆智能体维护动态轨迹记忆库，在上下文增长受限下保留早期移动模式并追踪行为变化；(2) 构建含全局视图与时间条件视图的多粒度时间感知类别转移图，保留被全局聚合掩盖的时间规律；(3) 类别预测智能体融合转移图与轨迹记忆预测下一类别；(4) 用 RQ-VAE 学习语义 ID 构建语义 POI 表示，并与类别引导的 POI 预测器端到端联合优化。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上，SHARP 相较最强基线平均提升 Acc@5 5.29 个百分点、Acc@10 4.52 个百分点。

Conclusion: 以类别级推理引导细粒度 POI 预测、结合动态记忆与时间感知转移图的分层智能体框架，可有效缓解语义鸿沟、时序演化与时间模式被平滑三类问题，显著提升下一 POI 预测性能。

Abstract: Next point-of-interest (POI) prediction supports personalized location services by anticipating users' next destinations. Recent methods leverage large language models (LLMs) for semantic reasoning and graphs for collective mobility modeling, improving POI prediction. However, three challenges still remain. Bridging LLMs' semantic reasoning and specific POI prediction remains challenging. User mobility also evolves over time, making it necessary to retain and update historical knowledge within limited LLM contexts. Meanwhile, global transition graphs smooth out time-specific category transition patterns. To address these challenges, we propose Semantic Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for Next POI Prediction with Dynamic Memory (SHARP), an agentic hierarchical framework in which category-level reasoning guides specific POI prediction. A trajectory memory agent maintains a dynamic trajectory memory bank that preserves earlier mobility patterns and tracks behavioral changes with limited context growth. We construct multi-granularity time-aware category transition graphs with global and time-conditioned views to preserve temporal regularities obscured by global aggregation. A category predict agent combines these graphs with trajectory memory to predict likely next categories. SHARP learns semantic IDs (SIDs) through a residual-quantized variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) to construct semantic POI representations, which are jointly optimized with a category-guided POI predictor end-to-end. On three real-world datasets, SHARP improves Acc@5 and Acc@10 by an average of 5.29 and 4.52 percentage points, respectively, over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [123] [FERN: Cross-Benchmark Table Retrieval for Text-to-SQL with Frozen Retrievers and Learned Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14477)
*Arjun Rao,Nishant Gopinath*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FERN 用三种冻结检索器融合出 100 个候选表，以 120 维特征和 LightGBM 排序器替代生成式 LLM 做表检索；在 Spider 上不微调即接近或超过 RASL 的表召回，但在分布外数据集上仍存在差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模合并 catalog 上的 Text-to-SQL 需先完成表检索。既有 RASL 依赖 Claude 3.5 等生成式模型抽取关键词并选表，成本高且依赖托管调用，作者希望用特征工程加学习排序的无生成式 LLM 方案替代。

Method: FERN（Feature-Engineered Ranking with No generative LLM）：将三个冻结检索器融合为 100 个候选表；为每个候选构造至多 120 维特征；用仅在 BIRD 训练集问题上一���拟合的 LightGBM 排序器重排。评测在合并的 BIRD、Spider、Fiben catalog 上进行，对照 RASL 公布的召回指标。

Result: Spider（未拟合）：候选池在 15 表处达到 RASL 的公布召回（98.0），排序器将 complete@5 从 83.9% 提升到 95.6%；5 表召回 96.7 对 97.0。BIRD：带 evidence 时 5 表召回 96.6 对 97.5，无 evidence 为 89.3（低 8.2 点）。BEAVER 121 题（未拟合）：15 表召回 82.1，介于两个 BEAVER 训练检索器（80.8、84.5）之间，但 complete@15 仅 49.6，低于 54.5 与 62.0。Fiben：15 表召回 54.2 对 69.2（题目集不同），50 表 80.7 对 79.4。BIRD dev 带 evidence 时，非检索特征使 complete@5 比仅用检索信号提高 7.7 点。单张 A100 上中位延迟 208 ms/题，无托管模型调用。

Conclusion: 特征工程加 LightGBM 排序可在无需生成式 LLM 的情况下，在领域内（BIRD）和邻近分布（Spider）达到接近 RASL 的表召回，且延迟低、无外部调用；但在完全分布外场景（BEAVER 的 complete@15、Fiben 的 15 表召回）泛化仍明显不足，且无 evidence 时对 BIRD 提示信息的依赖显著。非检索特征对完整召回贡献可观。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems over large pooled catalogs must first retrieve the tables a question needs. We compare against the published table recall of RASL, which uses Claude 3.5 models to extract keywords and select tables, on pooled BIRD, Spider, and Fiben catalogs. FERN (Feature-Engineered Ranking with No generative LLM) instead fuses three frozen retrievers into 100 candidates, describes each with up to 120 features, and reorders them with LightGBM rankers fitted once on BIRD's training questions. On Spider, where it was not fitted, its candidate pool alone reaches RASL's published recall at fifteen tables (98.0), and the ranker raises the share of questions with every gold table in the top five (complete@5) from 83.9% to 95.6%. At five tables, FERN's recall is within one point of RASL's on Spider (96.7 vs 97.0) and on BIRD with its evidence hints (96.6 vs 97.5), and 8.2 points below on BIRD without them (89.3). On 121 real BEAVER questions, without BEAVER fitting, its recall at fifteen (82.1) falls between two BEAVER-trained retrievers (80.8, 84.5), but its complete@15 is below both (49.6 vs 54.5 and 62.0). On Fiben, it falls far short at fifteen tables (54.2 vs 69.2, different question sets), although its recall at fifty is numerically close to RASL's question-only retrieval result (80.7 vs 79.4). On BIRD dev with evidence, non-retrieval features raise complete@5 by 7.7 points over retrieval signals alone. On BIRD dev, median latency is 208 ms per question on one A100, without hosted model calls.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Parallel Broyden Methods for Efficiently Evaluating Nonlinear State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13523)
*Ian Christopher Tanoh,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出基于Broyden方法的拟牛顿并行求解框架，用"对角+低秩"分块雅可比近似捕捉状态维度间耦合，从轨迹割线构造近似、免自动微分，并保持与并行scan的兼容性。理论证明压缩scan下的有限步精确恢复及由压缩误差与分块近似误差刻画的收敛性；在低秩RNN、并行MCMC与扩散去噪任务上迭代次数更少，在雅可比昂贵或状态维度大时是速度最快的并行方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性状态空间模型通常只能顺序评估，无法利用现代并行硬件。已有工作将其重构为求根问题并用并行牛顿法求解，但精确牛顿需稠密雅可比的高代价矩阵乘法；基于对角近似的拟牛顿法虽可扩展，却忽略状态维度间耦合而收敛缓慢。因此需要一个兼顾可扩展性与收敛速度的并行拟牛顿方案。

Method: 提出基于Broyden方法的拟牛顿方法：以"对角+低秩"近似雅可比各分块，捕捉耦合项；近似由轨迹割线构造，避免自动微分；通过秩压缩保持与并行scan兼容，并保留对角近似良好的计算复杂度扩展性。

Result: 理论证明压缩scan下可有限步恢复精确轨迹；收敛性由压缩误差与沿当前轨迹误差的分块近似误差（而非谱范数）追踪。实验在低秩RNN、并行MCMC算法和扩散去噪模型上收敛迭代次数少于其他拟牛顿法；在雅可比评估昂贵或状态维度大时，为速度最快的并行方法。

Conclusion: 对角+低秩的Broyden型拟牛顿并行求解在保持可扩展性的同时有效引入耦合信息，适用于交互近似低秩或雅可比评估昂贵的场景，优于现有并行拟牛顿方法。

Abstract: Nonlinear state space models are typically evaluated sequentially, limiting their ability to exploit modern parallel hardware. Recent work has shown that state space models can be evaluated in parallel by reformulating evaluation as a root-finding problem, which can be solved with a parallel form of Newton's method. However, exact Newton methods require expensive multiplication of dense Jacobian matrices, and quasi-Newton methods based on diagonal approximations, while more scalable, are often slow to converge because they neglect interactions across state dimensions. We introduce a novel quasi-Newton approach based on Broyden's method, which captures coupling terms with diagonal-plus-low-rank approximations to blocks of the Jacobian. We construct the approximations from trajectory secants, avoiding automatic differentiation while retaining the favorable scaling of diagonal approximations and remaining compatible with parallel scan through rank compression. We prove finite-step recovery of the exact trajectory under the compressed scan, and show that convergence is tracked by the compression error and by the block approximation error along the current trajectory error, rather than in spectral norm. Empirically, we show that our method is advantageous when interactions are approximately low rank or when Jacobian evaluation is expensive: on low-rank RNNs, parallel MCMC algorithms, and denoising diffusion models, it converges in fewer iterations than other quasi-Newton methods and is the fastest parallel method when Jacobian evaluation is expensive or state dimensions are large.

</details>


### [125] [An ensemble of graph generative models for assisted history matching of the Brugge field case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12927)
*Gleb Shishaev,Anton Shirokov,Shadfar Davoodi,Mohammed Al-Shargabi,Vasily Demyanov,Daniel Arnold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出基于分区分层图自编码器的辅助历史拟合方法，在Brugge基准油田上以160维联合潜空间配合CMA-ES优化，实现保持地质合理性的井级与油田级匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助历史拟合存在非唯一性：多套地质实现可给出等效的总体产量响应，但单井拟合差异显著；当油田包含多种沉积环境和建模策略时，这种不确定性进一步加剧。需在优化中约束解落于学习到的地质流形上。

Method: 针对Brugge基准模型的不同沉积/地层带，各带配置专用生成模型，共享一个联合潜空间用于历史拟合优化循环；图自编码器直接作用于角点网格的活跃网格单元，无需转换为规则格架即可处理不规则几何；在160维联合潜空间上用协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）搜索；目标函数为多分量，包含静态井数据失配、动态生产失配以及基于解码器诱导潜度量的地质合理性惩罚；并在历史拟合前用拓扑数据分析（TDA）诊断潜空间，判断单一生成器是否足够或需多生成器方案。

Result: 分区式建模给出连贯的潜空间，保留了随分区变化的孔隙度—渗透率主关系，并使解保持在学习到的地质流形内；拟合模型在先验不确定性范围内再现油田产油与产水，井级匹配质量与已有基于EnKF、PSO+PCA参数化、MKL+PSO的Brugge研究相当。

Conclusion: 分区分层的图自编码器联合潜空间历史拟合可有效缓解多沉积环境下的非唯一性，在保持地质一致性前提下获得与主流方法可比的拟合效果；TDA潜空间诊断可作为选择单/多生成器方案的依据。

Abstract: Assisted history matching remains non-unique because many geological realisations can reproduce equivalent production responses with substantial differences between individual well matches. This ambiguity increases when a field comprises several depositional settings and modelling strategies. The paper applied a graph autoencoder approach to history matching of a benchmark Brugge field that comprises different depositional zones. Each stratigraphic zone is modelled with its dedicated generative model that comprises a joint latent space for optimisation history matching loops. Graph-based generators operate directly on the active cells of the corner-point grid, so its irregular geometry is handled without conversion to a regular lattice. The optimisation search in the 160-dimensional joint latent space is done with the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy. The multi-component objective function includes static well data mismatch, dynamic production mismatch, and a geological realism penalty based on the Decoder induced latent metric. Latent space diagnostics with topological data analysis are applied prior to history matching to test whether one generator is sufficient or whether a multiple generator formulation is required. The results show that the zone-based formulation gives coherent latent spaces, preserves the main porosity and permeability relations that vary from one zone to another, and supports history matching while keeping the solutions within the learned geological manifold. The matched models reproduce field oil and water production within the prior uncertainty range and provide a comparable well-level match to previously published Brugge studies based on Ensemble Kalman Filter, Particle Swarm Optimisation with principal component analysis of parameterisation, and Multiple Kernel Learning with Particle Swarm Optimisation.

</details>


### [126] [Multifidelity Operator Inference for Non-intrusive Model Reduction under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13624)
*Vivian Zhang,Tomoki Koike,Atticus Rex,Elizabeth Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多保真算子推断(MF-OpInf)，利用大量低保真数据补充稀缺高保真快照，通过多保真线性回归估计降阶算子，降低方差并在相同预算下提升PDE解场预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计与不确定性量化中的多查询分析在高保真PDE模型下代价高昂。机器学习代理模型(尤其非侵入的算子推断OpInf)虽有吸引力，但受计算预算限制，高保真训练数据稀缺，导致OpInf模型方差大，这在高风险工程设计中是严重隐患。

Method: 设计MF-OpInf：以同一问题较粗离散得到的丰富低保真数据补充稀缺高保真快照；采用多保真线性回归框架，跨不同保真度数据共同估计降阶算子。该方法保持非侵入性。

Result: 在若干模型PDE问题上的数值实验表明，在相同计算预算下，MF-OpInf对PDE解场的预测精度高于仅使用高保真数据的OpInf。

Conclusion: 多保真数据融合能有效缓解OpInf在小样本高保真训练下的高方差问题，在相等预算下获得更准确的降阶代理模型。

Abstract: Many-query analyses, in which a computational model is repeatedly evaluated, play an important role in uncertainty quantification and optimization for engineering design. However, many-query analyses can be prohibitively expensive when considering high-fidelity numerical models, especially those governed by partial differential equations (PDEs). Surrogate modeling replaces costly simulations with cheap approximations, enabling practical uncertainty quantification and design workflows. Machine learning (ML) approaches to surrogate modeling have gained significant traction in the development of surrogate models due to their non-intrusive nature; they do not require access to the underlying simulation code. The Operator Inference (OpInf) approach, in particular, fits reduced operators to state and corresponding time derivative data. However, high-fidelity training data is scarce due to limited computational budgets, and OpInf models trained on such scarce data often exhibit high variance, a critical liability in high-risk engineering design. We propose Multifidelity Operator Inference (MF-OpInf), which reduces this variance by supplementing scarce high-fidelity snapshots with abundant low-fidelity data drawn from coarser discretizations of the same problem. MF-OpInf handles data across varying fidelity levels by utilizing a multifidelity linear regression framework to estimate the reduced operators. Numerical experiments on model PDE problems demonstrate that MF-OpInf predicts PDE solution fields with higher accuracy compared to the high-fidelity-only OpInf with the same computational budget.

</details>


### [127] [Predictive Surprise as Self-Grounding Concept Bottleneck for Interpretable Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12955)
*Sachith Abeywickrama,Emadeldeen Eldele,Min Wu,Xiaoli Li,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ConceptTime，用冻结概率预测器的"预测意外度"作为单一自监督信号，同时完成时序分段与概念表征，实现准确、可解释、可弃权的时序分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感领域的时序分类器需同时满足准确、可解释、证据不足时可弃权三点，但"分段需知模式、模式需知边界"的循环依赖使现有基于固定窗口或专家词表的方法至少牺牲其中一项；该障碍是结构性的而非工程性的。

Method: 冻结的概率预测器在每个时间步输出下一观测的分布，预测与实际信号的失配即"预测意外度"；该信号既定位分段边界，又借助预测器刻画各段（以相对预期的行为而非外观为依据）。这些段摘要聚类成动力学机制词表（概念），由轻量分类头基于概念序列分类，并以到最近概念的距离天然给出可靠性/弃权。

Result: 在17个公开基准上与SOTA黑盒方法准确率相当，优于所有可解释基线；Epilepsy 仅用1%标签仍保持近98%准确率；在OOD检测与删除忠实性上与小softmax、原型距离及SHAP/TimeX/TimeX++保持竞争力，且无需任何标注、语言模型调用或领域专家。

Conclusion: 以预测意外度闭环打破分段-模式循环，ConceptTime 在无监督条件下同时兼顾准确、可解释与可弃权，为风险敏感时序分类提供了统一范式。

Abstract: Time-series classifiers deployed in risk-sensitive domains must be (1) accurate, (2) interpretable, and (3) able to abstain when evidence is weak. Existing methods do not deliver all 3 at once, and the obstacle is structural, rather than logistical. Meaningful temporal patterns cannot be defined without knowing segment boundaries, yet meaningful boundaries cannot be placed without knowing which patterns to expect. Existing approaches break this circularity by using fixed windows or expert-annotated vocabularies, thereby sacrificing at least one of the 3 properties. We introduce ConceptTime, which closes the loop through a single self-supervised signal. A frozen probabilistic forecaster predicts a distribution over the next observation at every timestep of the sequence. We call the mismatch between its prediction and the realized signal the predictive surprise. This signal both locates segment boundaries and characterizes each resulting segment through the forecaster. Segments are thus grounded by how they behaved relative to expectation, not by what they look like. These summaries cluster into a vocabulary of dynamical regimes that we call concepts. A lightweight head classifies from the concept sequence, and the distance to the nearest concept yields reliability by design. Across 17 public benchmarks, ConceptTime is competitive with state-of-the-art black-box accuracy, beats every interpretable baseline, and retains near-98% accuracy on Epilepsy with 1% of labels. It further remains competitive with softmax, prototype-distance, and SHAP/TimeX/TimeX++ on OOD detection and deletion faithfulness, without a single annotation, language model call, or domain expert. Code is available at: https://github.com/Sachithx/ConceptTime.git.

</details>


### [128] [Geometry-Dependent Approximation and Stability Properties of Shallow ReLU$^k$ Networks on Bounded Euclidean Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13952)
*Yuqing Liu,Tong Mao,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究有界欧氏区域上隐藏参数几何对浅层ReLU^k网络的影响，以填充距离h和反对极分离距离h̄刻画参数几何，给出逼近速率、饱和型下界、Gram/刚度矩阵谱、Bernstein与Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund不等式、离散最小二乘恢复及反逼近等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在量化网络参数几何（而非仅网络规模）如何决定浅层ReLU^k网络的逼近精度、系数表示的稳定性、离散恢复及收敛速率中所含的 regularity 信息。

Method: 采用光滑局部化与二进Fourier估计；用填充距离h与反对极分离距离h̄刻画参数几何；在预设网络空间L_n^k上导出上界逼近估计（利用目标 regularity 至临界阶(d+2k+1)/2）与渐近饱和型下界（针对更光滑的固定目标）；并给出依赖几何的谱估计、范数离散化与正则化恢复、以及基于系数或分离条件的反逼近分析。

Result: 上界：inf‖f-g_n‖_{L²(Ω')} ≲ h^r‖f‖_{H^r(Ω)}，在内子域Ω'上成立；下界：inf‖f-g_n‖_{L²(Ω)} ≳ h̄^{(d+2k+1)/2}‖f‖_{L²(Ω')}，对光滑度超过临界阶的固定目标渐近成立；并获得几何依赖的Gram与刚度矩阵谱估计（刻画条件数与系数稳定性）、Bernstein与Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund不等式、离散最小二乘恢复及反逼近的 regularity 推断结果。

Conclusion: 参数几何（h与h̄）系统性地支配浅层ReLU^k网络的逼近精度、系数表示稳定性、离散恢复质量及收敛速率所承载的 regularity 信息，上界受临界阶(d+2k+1)/2限制并呈饱和现象。

Abstract: On bounded Euclidean domains, we study how hidden-parameter geometry affects shallow ReLU$^k$ networks through results on approximation rates, saturation-type lower bounds, Gram and stiffness matrix spectra, Bernstein and Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund inequalities, discrete least-squares recovery, and inverse approximation.
  Our analysis uses smooth localization and dyadic Fourier estimates. We quantify parameter geometry by the fill distance $h$ and antipodal separation distance $\underline h$. For the prescribed network space $L_n^k$, representative approximation estimates take the form \begin{equation*} \begin{aligned} \inf_{g_n\in L_n^k}\|f-g_n\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω')}&\lesssim h^r\|f\|_{\mathcal H^r(Ω)},\\ \inf_{g_n\in L_n^k}\|f-g_n\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω)}&\gtrsim \underline h^{\frac{d+2k+1}{2}}\|f\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω')}. \end{aligned} \end{equation*} The upper bound exploits target regularity up to the critical order $\frac{d+2k+1}{2}$, while the lower bound applies asymptotically to fixed targets smoother than this order. The upper estimate holds on an interior subdomain $Ω'$, whereas the lower estimate concerns the larger domain $Ω$.
  We also obtain geometry-dependent spectral estimates that quantify conditioning and coefficient stability. Further results establish norm discretization and regularized recovery from function values, and infer target regularity from approximation rates under coefficient or separation conditions. Altogether, these results describe how parameter geometry governs approximation accuracy, the stability of coefficient representations, discrete recovery, and the regularity information contained in convergence rates.

</details>


### [129] [ADIndex: Layer-Adaptive Sparse Attention Indexing via Group-Level Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12956)
*Jin-Fei Qi,Yiduo Li,Jiang-Xin Shi,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Hanlin Tang,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADIndex 通过层间自适应校准、组级蒸馏和低延迟推理策略改造 DeepSeek 稀疏注意力的 indexer，在保持长上下文任务性能近无损的同时显著加速 prefill 与解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek Sparse Attention 依赖 token 级选择降低注意力计算，但其 indexer 仍需为每个 query 对完整因果前缀打分，在长上下文下成为延迟主要来源——长上下文时 indexer 单独可占 prefill GPU 时间的 50% 以上。同时作者观察到显著的层间异质性。

Method: 提出 ADIndex，含三部分：(i) 层级校准策略，依据跨 query 检索重叠自适应调整，利用观察到的层间异质性；(ii) 组级蒸馏方案，据此训练自适应的层分组 indexer，捕捉单 query 的关键检索信号；(iii) 低延迟推理策略，通过 query 组内 token bank 共享减少重复的全前缀扫描，同时保留 query 级精化。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V3.2 与 GLM-5.1 上，LongBench、LongBench v2、RULER 任务性能近无损；相比标准 DSA，256K 上下文下分层复用配置的 prefill 与解码 kernel 加速最高达 6.22x 与 3.40x，128K 上下文下端到端服务加速 1.54x。

Conclusion: 索引阶段是长上下文稀疏注意力推理的关键瓶颈，利用层间异质性的自适应分组与蒸馏式索引设计可在近乎不掉点的前提下带来大幅效率提升。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) reduces the computational cost of attention through token-level selection. However, its indexer still scores the entire causal prefix for each query, making indexing a dominant source of latency in long-context inference. Notably, the indexer alone can account for more than 50% of total prefill GPU time at long context lengths. To address this bottleneck, we propose ADIndex, an Adaptive and Distillation-driven Indexing method for token-level sparse attention. ADIndex integrates three key components: (i) a layer-wise calibration strategy that adaptively accommodates cross-query retrieval overlap, motivated by our observation of pronounced inter-layer heterogeneity; (ii) a group-level distillation scheme that leverages this heterogeneity to efficiently train adaptive layer-wise group indexers, capturing salient retrieval signals from individual queries; and (iii) a low-latency inference strategy that reduces repeated full-prefix scans through token bank sharing within query groups while retaining query-specific refinement. Experiments on DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5.1 demonstrate that ADIndex preserves near-lossless task performance on LongBench, LongBench v2, and RULER while delivering substantial efficiency gains over standard DSA, including prefill and decoding kernel speedups of up to 6.22x and 3.40x, respectively, at a context length of 256K across the layer-wise reuse configurations, as well as an end-to-end serving speedup of 1.54x at a context length of 128K.

</details>


### [130] [Exploration via Exploitation: The Blessing of Reward Diversity in Personalized Federated RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12964)
*Jiin Woo,Gauri Joshi,Yuejie Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦强化学习中的奖励异质性从"共识障碍"重新定位为"探索性资产"：先提出PF-UCBVI实现解耦共享动态与个性化目标的最优线性加速，再提出无显式探索的纯贪心算法PF-EFVI，证明充分奖励多样性下可达对数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦强化学习假设同质环境以保证高效率，而智能体目标不一致（奖励异质）通常被视为协作的障碍。作者反其道而行，希望论证奖励异质性并非阻碍，而是一种能促进探索的结构性资源，从而在个性化目标下仍实现高效协同学习。

Method: (1) 提出个性化联邦UCB值迭代算法PF-UCBVI，通过将共享动态与个性化目标解耦，实现最优线性加速；(2) 进一步提出PF-EFVI，一种纯贪心、无显式探索的价值迭代算法，利用奖励多样性本身保证状态-动作覆盖，并给出在充分奖励多样性条件下达到对数遗憾的理论证明。

Result: PF-UCBVI获得最优线性加速；PF-EFVI在无显式探索奖励的情况下达到对数遗憾。结果表明智能体之间的目标分歧将探索负担从时间维度的复杂度转移到空间维度的多样性上。

Conclusion: 奖励异质性可以被转化为探索的结构性优势，使得无需显式探索即可实现高效的集体学习；这为个性化联邦强化学习提供了新的理论视角与算法范式。

Abstract: While federated reinforcement learning (RL) has traditionally guaranteed high efficiency in homogeneous environments, collaboration is often hindered when agents possess distinct goals. In this paper, we flip this paradigm, framing reward heterogeneity not as a hurdle to consensus, but as a structural asset that catalyzes exploration. We first propose Personalized Federated Upper Confidence Bound Value Iteration (PF-UCBVI), which achieves optimal linear speedup by decoupling shared dynamics from personalized objectives. To eliminate the risks of explicit exploration, we then introduce Personalized Federated Exploration-Free Value Iteration (PF-EFVI), a purely greedy algorithm that leverages reward diversity to ensure state-action coverage. We prove that PF-EFVI attains logarithmic regret without explicit exploration bonuses under sufficient reward diversity. Our results reveal that agent disagreement shifts the exploration burden from temporal complexity to spatial diversity, enabling efficient collective learning without explicit exploration.

</details>


### [131] [StatPrompt-LLM: A Lightweight Statistical Refinement for Foundation-Style Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12974)
*Aniket Sinha,Veronica W. Tsou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 StatPrompt-LLM，在 Time-LLM 的提示机制中注入数据集级统计信息，并引入零初始化自回归旁路作为线性预测通路，以在观测有限时将趋势、尺度与时序依赖等基本时间结构显式暴露给大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型式时间序列预测虽已将大型预训练语言架构迁移至序列数据，但如何有效向模型暴露趋势、尺度、时序依赖等基本时间结构仍未被解决，尤其在可用观测数据有限的情形下更为突出。

Method: 对不同代际的预测架构开展探索性实证研究；在 Time-LLM 基础上进行轻量级统计精炼：增强其提示机制以融入数据集级统计信息，并加入零初始化的自回归旁路作为互补的线性预测路径。在 ETTh1、ETTh2、Weather 等电力与气象基准上，于全前缀与缩减前缀两种配置下评估。

Result: 在 10% 缩减前缀配置下，StatPrompt-LLM 的样本内预测误差低于 Time-LLM，跨数据集未加权平均相对降幅为 MSE 40.2%、MAE 21.4%。全前缀结果更依赖数据集。消融显示在组合架构中屏蔽部分额外统计描述子组时变化相对有限且依赖数据集。计算分析表明相对 Time-LLM 存在额外参数量与运行时开销。

Conclusion: 统计先验提示与线性旁路的轻量组合在数据受限场景下显著改善预测精度，但收益随数据完整性而变、统计描述子贡献边际有限，且以额外参数量与计算开销为代价。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting has recently seen a paradigm shift with the introduction of foundation-style models that adapt large pretrained language architectures to sequential data. However, effectively exposing fundamental temporal structures, such as trend, scale, and temporal dependence, to these models remains an open challenge, particularly when the available observations are limited. In this paper, we conduct an exploratory empirical study across different generations of forecasting architectures and propose StatPrompt-LLM, a lightweight statistical refinement of Time-LLM. StatPrompt-LLM augments the existing Time-LLM prompting mechanism with dataset-level statistical information and introduces a zero-initialised autoregressive bypass as a complementary linear forecasting pathway. We evaluate our framework on electricity and meteorological time-series benchmarks, including ETTh1, ETTh2, and Weather, under full-prefix and reduced-prefix configurations. Under the 10% reduced-prefix configuration, StatPrompt-LLM achieved lower in-sample prediction errors than Time-LLM, with unweighted average relative reductions across datasets of 40.2% in MSE and 21.4% in MAE. Full-prefix results were more dataset-dependent. Ablation analyses revealed relatively modest and dataset-dependent changes when selected additional statistical descriptor groups were masked within the combined architecture. Computational profiling indicated additional model parameters and runtime overhead relative to Time-LLM.

</details>


### [132] [MADSNet: A Multi-Scale Axial-Attentive Dense Spiking Network for Spike Camera Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12980)
*Sirui Li,Rui Zhao,Siqi Cai,Haizhou Li,Tiejun Huang,Yajing Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MADSNet——一种多尺度轴向注意力密集脉冲网络，用于脉冲相机图像重建，在保持SNN低功耗低延迟优势的同时显著提升重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲相机以高时间分辨率的二值脉冲流编码动态场景，SNN可凭借稀疏、仅累加运算高效处理此类信号，但现有基于SNN的重建方法普遍网络较浅，重建质量落后于ANN方法。

Method: MADSNet采用分层脉冲编码器—解码器架构：多尺度脉冲stem提取早期特征；轴向脉冲注意力捕获长程依赖；密集脉冲瓶颈增强特征复用；脉冲—连续解码器将脉冲域表示还原为连续强度图像。

Result: 在灰度与彩色脉冲重建实验中，SREDS上较SSIR提升4.3 dB PSNR、较Spk2ImgNet提升1.94 dB；相比Spk2ImgNet延迟约降低15倍，估计能耗仅约1/40。

Conclusion: 精心设计的SNN架构能够在保留计算效率（低延迟、低能耗）的同时提升重建质量，说明SNN在脉冲相机视觉中具备与ANN竞争乃至超越的潜力。

Abstract: Spike cameras encode dynamic scenes into high-temporal-resolution binary spike streams for efficient high-speed vision. Although spiking neural networks (SNNs) can process such signals with sparse and accumulate-only computation, existing SNN-based reconstruction methods are typically shallow and still lag behind ANN-based models in reconstruction fidelity. This paper proposes MADSNet, a Multi-scale Axial-attentive Dense Spiking Network for spike camera image reconstruction. MADSNet adopts a hierarchical spiking encoder--decoder, where a multi-scale spike stem extracts early features, axial spike attention captures long-range dependencies, a dense spiking bottleneck enhances feature reuse, and a spike-to-continuous decoder reconstructs continuous intensity images from spike-domain representations. Experiments on gray-scale and color spike reconstruction show that MADSNet outperforms SSIR by 4.3 dB PSNR and Spk2ImgNet by 1.94 dB on SREDS. Compared with Spk2ImgNet, it achieves about 15$\times$ lower latency and uses only about 1/40 of the estimated energy, demonstrating that carefully designed SNN architectures can improve reconstruction quality while preserving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [133] [Third Eye: Beyond Immediate Gains in Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12988)
*Sujato Dutta,Padala Indira Sneha,Kypu Mahitha,Shashank Vanga,Neeraj Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"第三只眼"(Third Eye)评测框架，将自我更新(self-update)的即时表现与再经历一次原生更新后的持续效用相区分，发现即时看似中性的自我更新在后续更新后会产生约1.78个百分点的效用下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多以自我更新后的即时性能衡量其价值，但忽略了模型再次更新后该更新是否仍然有效。作者质疑"即时收益能否等同于完整价值"，主张把持续自我更新视为适应过程（adaptation transitions）而非一次性增益。

Method: 在5个3-8B骨干模型（Qwen3-4B、Llama-3.2-3B、SmolLM3-3B、Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B）上，于数学与代码任务进行评测：64次全新运行，每次运行产生3个候选，每个候选做3次续接(continuation)。选择集与最终评分集使用互不重叠的样本；采用平衡式Qwen3-4B/Llama-3.2-3B对比、同步95%区间、候选平均即时效用、目标分布与分布外损失分解，以及匹配配方和接受方-贡献方(recepient-donor)对照实验。

Result: 在平衡对比中，即时选出的效用接近零，但续接使其再降低1.78个百分点（同步95%区间[0.68, 2.88]）；候选平均即时效用已为负，说明部分"中性"首步源于选择效应。续接在SmolLM3-3B与Llama-3.1-8B上同样降低效用，Qwen3-8B结论不确定。目标分布与OOD损失主导了保持率的变化；匹配配方与接受方-贡献方对照既未找到可靠缓解手段，也未识别出单一机制。

Conclusion: 应将持续自我更新作为适应过程来评估，配合独立评分集和无更新基线，而不应把即时性能视为其全部价值。

Abstract: Does a useful self-update remain useful after the model updates again? Third Eye separates immediate performance from the outcome of a further native update. We evaluate five 3-8B backbones on math and code: 64 fresh runs, three candidates per run, and three continuations per candidate. Selection and final scoring use disjoint items. In the balanced Qwen3-4B/Llama-3.2-3B comparison, immediate-selected utility is near zero, but continuation reduces it by 1.78 percentage points (simultaneous 95% interval [0.68, 2.88]). Candidate-averaged immediate utility is already negative: selection partly accounts for the apparently neutral first step. Continuation also lowers utility for SmolLM3-3B and Llama-3.1-8B; Qwen3-8B remains inconclusive. Target and out-of-distribution losses dominate retention changes. Matched recipe and recipient-donor controls identify neither a reliable mitigation nor a single mechanism. The results support evaluating persistent self-updates as adaptation transitions, with independent scoring and a no-update baseline, rather than interpreting immediate performance as their full value.

</details>


### [134] [Multimodal Graph Learning: A Survey of Models, Benchmarks, and Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12990)
*Rui Xue,Tianfu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述以"算法中心+评测导向"视角系统梳理多模态属性图（MAG）学习，提出统一消息传递框架并将方法按特征、传播、拓扑、学习、基础模型五个集成层级组织，同时综述基准数据集与评测协议，并展望与AI智能体的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAG广泛支撑电商、社交媒体、内容平台等应用，但其研究分化为图中心方法与基础模型中心方法两条路线，且各类基准数据集与评测协议互不统一，缺乏统一的形式化框架与可比的评测视角。

Method: 采用算法中心与评测导向的分析框架，做三方面工作：(1) 提出统一的消息传递公式，将代表性方法推导为其特例，按五个集成层级（特征、传播、拓扑、学习、基础模型）分类，比较其假设、表达力与开销，并识别"跨模态敏感性"作为区分相似架构的关键属性；(2) 梳理近期基准，总结数据集与指标，定义与模型无关的描述子（标签同质性、平均度、聚类系数、边来源），并将评测协议映射到其使用的基线；(3) 连接AI智能体，讨论智能体感知与记忆的多模态图以及"图工程"，并提出开放问题与研究方向。

Result: 建立了一个统一的消息传递形式化体系，可将判别式与图条件生成式方法纳入同一框架并作为特例导出；给出了涵盖五个集成层级的方法分类与对比；汇总了基准数据集、指标及模型无关图描述子，并将评测协议与基线对应；指出跨模态敏感性是区分相似架构的关键性质；明确了在强多模态基础模型背景下图结构何时有益的开放问题。

Conclusion: MAG学习需要统一的算法形式化与标准化评测，以支撑跨方法的可比分析；未来应在基础模型与图结构的协同、智能体感知与记忆的多模态图以及图工程等方向推进，并建立社区可复用的评测基础。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs), in which nodes carry text, images, and other modalities while edges encode relations among entities, underlie e-commerce, social media, content platforms, and creative applications. Learning on MAGs has developed along two complementary lines: graph-centric methods redesign how modalities are fused, propagated, and connected within message passing, while foundation-model-centric methods pretrain transferable multimodal graph encoders or bring graph context into multimodal large language models for prediction and generation. In parallel, several benchmarks have appeared with different datasets and evaluation protocols. This survey takes an algorithm-centric and evaluation-grounded view of the field and makes three contributions. Modeling: we introduce a unified message-passing formulation and organize methods into five integration levels (feature, propagation, topology, learning, and foundation model), covering discriminative and graph-conditioned generative models; within this formulation we derive representative methods as special cases, compare their assumptions, expressiveness, and cost, and identify cross-modal sensitivity as a property that separates otherwise similar architectures. Datasets and evaluation: we review recent benchmarks, summarize their datasets and metrics, define model-independent descriptors (label homophily, average degree, clustering, and edge provenance), and map each evaluation protocol to the baselines it uses. Frontiers: we connect multimodal graph learning to AI agents, covering the multimodal graphs that agents perceive and remember and graph engineering, the practice of organizing agent workflows as explicit graphs; we then discuss open problems, concrete efforts for the community, and research directions, including when graph structure helps in the presence of strong multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [135] [INTENT: Out-of-Sample, Out-of-Time, and Cold-Start Evaluation on User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13069)
*Mattias Kvist,Erik Lidman Hillbom,Edoardo de Cal,Davis Siemens,Adam Fredriksson,Rickard Brüel-Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出公开基准INTENT，按"用户/时段/目标物品是否在训练中出现"划分七个评估单元，审计59个公开数据集家族并保留5个，统一切分规则，揭示不同单元下最优模型不同且"重复上一物品"这一无参基线在多数数据集新物品上领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行等在有序客户事件序列上训练基础模型并做前向预测，常需面向训练中未出现的客户与商户；但各机构在私有数据上评估，结果不可比。现有公开基准很少测试模型未见过的时段，推荐研究通常仅留出每个用户的最后若干事件，无法刻画"新用户/新时段/新物品"带来的泛化差距。

Method: 构建INTENT基准（INTEraction, Novelty, and Transfer evaluation for next-action prediction），按用户、时段、目标物品是否在训练中出现定义7个评估单元；对59个公开数据集家族做审计并保留5个（最常见缺陷是缺少可排序事件的时间戳）；因无日期落在五个数据集共同时间跨度内，各数据集按自身截断点切分，切分规则在评估任何模型前固定；各单元用户数合计均≥111,769。

Result: 参考结果中评估单元决定最优模型：Transformer在截断后已见物品上领先，但在新物品上得分为零；融合物品元数据的变体主要仅在MicroLens上有效；"重复用户上一物品"的无参基线在5个数据集中的4个于新物品上领先。该基线还表明，在3个数据集上截断后分数下降主要源于行为变化而非模型能力。

Conclusion: 评估单元而非单一总分应成为模型选择依据，需区分已见/新物品及时间漂移；无参基线作为必要对照暴露模型对新物品的失效；流水线、切分规则与各单元用户数已公开，便于可比复现。

Abstract: Banks now train foundation models on ordered sequences of customer events such as transactions, app sessions, and authorizations. Deployed, such a model predicts forward in time, often for customers and merchants absent from training, yet each is evaluated on private data, so no two can be compared. Public benchmarks for such sequences rarely test a period the model has not seen; recommendation studies usually hold out each user's last events instead.
