CatBoost, TabM, and FT-Transformer reduce RMSE to 1.3-1.6 m/s, against approximately 3 m/s for power-law and Monin-Obukhov fits; once the predictor set is fixed the three architectures differ by less than 0.05 m/s, so accuracy is driven by predictors rather than architecture. A cohort analysis of within-bin profile dispersion places an empirical floor of approximately 1.77 m/s on any point-wise predictor that uses standard radiosonde features, consistent with prior eddy-viscosity inversions. A one-dimensional Fourier Neural Operator applied as a post-hoc corrector to frozen tabular predictions reduces near-surface RMSE on U.S. stations from 1.5 to 1.2 m/s, while leaving upper-layer error unchanged. Further gains should come from new predictors rather than larger models.

</details>


### [169] [Looping Deeper Without Growing the KV Cache: Recurrent Depth for Causal Encoder-Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13378)
*Jiaqi Leng,Minshen Zhang,Zhaofeng Sun,Yu-Yang Qian,Peng Zhao,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Looped Causal Encoder-Decoder (Looped CED) 通过在编码器侧循环并以定点均衡实现跨层跨轮KV共享，实现在不增参数、不增KV缓存的条件下获得循环深度收益，并在552B模型上验证了大规模服务可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型可在不增加参数的情况下加深计算，但跨层、跨轮缓存完整上下文KV状态会严重限制服务batch size，需要解决"在非对称编码器-解码器栈中何处、如何循环"的问题。

Method: 设计循环因果编码器-解码器架构：仅稀疏编码器层生成全局KV，循环置于编码器以迭代精炼全局KV表示（而非重读静态解码器投影）；训练时采用随机循环深度，诱导定点均衡，使每轮可无损共享最后一轮的KV缓存；并将跨层与跨轮KV共享叠加。

Result: Looped CED达到与未循环CED相同的最小内存占用，以1/6的全局KV恢复循环Transformer 86-93%的收益；在552B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash上8轮循环于单节点4xGB200：逐层逐轮KV无法容纳单个128K请求，而Looped CED每token KV少36.8倍，可容纳38个此类请求（与未循环模型相当），8K提示下吞吐提升3.6倍。

Conclusion: 循环应放在编码器侧以精炼全局KV，且随机深度训练可使轮间KV无损共享；该设计在保持长上下文扩展性与语言建模增益的同时，实现了零轮次KV增长和工业级规模的高效服务。

Abstract: Looped language models deepen computation without adding parameters, yet caching full-context key-value (KV) states across layers and rounds severely bounds serving batch size. We design a looped causal encoder-decoder (CED) that achieves recurrent depth with zero round-wise KV growth, addressing where and how to loop an asymmetric stack in which only sparse encoder layers generate global KV. We find that recurrence must be placed in the encoder to iteratively refine global KV representations rather than re-reading static decoder projections, which both drives language-modeling gains and preserves long-context scaling. Furthermore, training the encoder loop under stochastic recurrence depth induces a fixed-point equilibrium where every round losslessly shares the last round's KV cache. Compounding cross-layer and cross-round KV sharing, the resulting Looped CED matches an unlooped CED's minimal memory footprint and recovers 86-93% of a looped Transformer's gain with 1/6 of its global KV. To test serving at production scale, we loop the 552B-parameter DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash for 8 rounds on one 4xGB200 node: with per-layer and per-round KV the loop cannot fit a single 128K-token request, whereas Looped CED holds 36.8x less KV per token, fits 38 such requests (as many as the unlooped model), and serves 3.6x higher throughput on 8K-token prompts.

</details>


### [170] [Settling the Burn-in Gap: Sharp Risk Bounds for Tail-Averaged SGD in Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13383)
*Qingyue Zhao,Xuheng Li,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在高维线性回归中研究带尾平均的常量步长在线SGD的超额风险，基于实例相关的谱框架给出与初始化显式匹配的上下界，并指出既有上下界均不紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zou等(2021)的谱分析中有效方差存在根本性缺口：上界保留了头部特征空间初始化误差的未衰减贡献，而下界却完全遗漏了头部初始化诱导的方差，二者不匹配。

Method: 对尾平均SGD的超额风险建立初始化显式的匹配上下界，以总体协方差谱H与初始化w0-w*表示，在轨迹谱反馈条件下成立；并证明该条件在两种常量步长情形下满足：(i) 任意协方差谱与有限时域，采用执行前选定的时域相关步长；(ii) 多项式衰减谱λ_i≍i^{-a}(a>1)，采用单一与时域无关的正步长。

Result: 单跳快模态的初始化方差在burn-in阶段指数衰减，而早期burn-in步骤中从头部分散到慢谱模态的四阶矩能量则以未衰减形式幸存进入平均窗口；并构造一维与二维的分离实例，证明Zou等(2021)下界存在指数对多项式的差距。

Conclusion: 既有上下界均松散；仅凭四阶矩假设无法确定渐近指数衰减速率，需附加结构性条件。

Abstract: We study the excess risk of constant-stepsize online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with tail-averaging for high-dimensional linear regression. Building on the instance-dependent spectral framework of Zou et al. (2021), we establish matching initialization-explicit upper and lower bounds on the total excess risk of tail-averaged SGD in terms of the population covariance spectrum $\mathbf{H}$ and initialization $\mathbf{w}_0-\mathbf{w}^*$ whenever a trajectory spectral feedback condition holds. Existing analyses exhibit a fundamental gap in the effective variance: the upper bound of Zou et al. (2021) retains an undamped contribution from the initial error in the head eigenspace $\mathbf{I}_{0:k^\dagger}$, whereas their lower bound omits the variance induced by the head initialization altogether. We show that both the existing upper and lower bounds are loose: the single-hop fast-mode variance contribution from the head initialization is exponentially damped during the burn-in phase, whereas fourth-moment energy scattered from the head initialization into slow spectral modes during the early burn-in steps survives undamped into the averaging window. We prove that our spectral feedback condition is satisfied in two concrete constant-stepsize regimes: (i) for arbitrary covariance spectra $\mathbf{H}$ and any finite horizon, under an explicit horizon-dependent stepsize chosen prior to execution; and (ii) for polynomially decaying spectra $λ_i\asymp i^{-a}$ ($a>1$), under a single horizon-independent positive stepsize. Finally, we complement these positive results with explicit one- and two-dimensional separation constructions proving an exponential-versus-polynomial gap in the lower bound of Zou et al. (2021) and showing why fourth-moment assumptions alone cannot determine the asymptotic exponential decay rate without additional structural conditions.

</details>


### [171] [AdjointFlow: Accelerating Scientific Inverse Problems with Physics-Based Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13389)
*Ethan Hanold,Nicholas M. Boffi,Qin Li,Leonardo Zepeda-Núñez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AdjointFlow，基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）将已知物理模型注入生成式传输过程本身，实现逆问题的高精度、低推理成本重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆问题广泛存在于地震成像、天体物理、无损检测等领域，但经典求解器面临非凸性与昂贵物理模型重复求解的问题；扩散模型可离线分摊计算，但纯数据驱动的条件模型未充分利用已知物理，且当目标分布集中时传输映射病态；PnP 方法虽利用物理模型提升数据一致性，却重新引入昂贵的迭代计算。

Method: 基于随机插值框架，将物理模型嵌入生成传输：(1) 设计数据依赖的、基于伴随（adjoint）的初始化，缩短到目标的传输距离；(2) 修正传输速度场，使端点分布集中时仍保持正则性；(3) 参数化网络，将初始化直接内建于学习到的速度场中；另提供可选的推理时细化，通过查询物理模型提升数据一致性且无需重训练。

Result: 在逆散射、地震成像、黑洞成像实验中，AdjointFlow 的重建精度一致优于条件扩散模型，推理速度最高比 PnP 方法快五个数量级；可选细化可将数据一致性提升至与 PnP 相当或更优。

Conclusion: 将已知物理注入生成式传输本身而非仅在推理阶段施加约束，可同时获得高精度与低推理成本，为物理引导的生成式逆问题求解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Inverse problems are ubiquitous across scientific applications ranging from seismic imaging to astrophysics to nondestructive testing, yet classical solvers often struggle with nonconvexity and repeated evaluations of expensive physical models. Diffusion models can amortize this computation offline, but largely data-driven conditional models underutilize known physics and suffer from ill-conditioned transport maps when the target distribution is concentrated. Plug-and-play (PnP) methods explicitly leverage the physical model to guide a learned prior, improving data consistency but reintroducing costly iterative computations. We instead propose AdjointFlow, a framework built on stochastic interpolants that injects the known physics into the generative transport itself. We design a data-dependent, adjoint-based initialization to reduce transport distance to the target, modify the transport velocity to remain regular as the endpoint distributions concentrate, and parameterize the network to build the initialization directly into the learned velocity. Together, these enable accurate reconstruction at substantially lower cost than generative models starting from Gaussian noise. Experiments on inverse scattering, seismic imaging, and black hole imaging show that AdjointFlow consistently produces more accurate reconstructions than conditional diffusion models while being up to five orders of magnitude faster at inference than PnP methods. Optional inference-time refinement that queries the physical model can further improve data consistency, matching or exceeding the accuracy of PnP methods without retraining.

</details>


### [172] [Free Lunch Uncertainty: Sequence-Level Rényi Entropy from Particle Decoding for Adaptive LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13414)
*Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Milad Heidari Sorkhehdizaj,Shahab Rahimirad,Aliasghar Pourghani,Seyedarmin Azizi,Souvik Kundu,Abolfazl Hashemi,Joseph Campbell,Vijay Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出SMC幂采样解码已估计幂分布归一化常数，可零边际成本得到序列级Rényi熵(SRE)，并提出Rényi链式法则扩展到任意阶逐步熵；1/2阶成员累积每步搜索对数代价，是13个设置中10个最优的零成本选择预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对整个LLM补全的不确定性量化对选择性预测与自适应计算至关重要，但现有零成本信号（平均token熵、对数概率）仅局部于token或单个补全，自一致性需额外多次补全，代价高。

Method: 利用SMC幂采样解码已估计幂分布归一化常数的性质，零额外开销读出序列级Rényi熵；用Rényi链式法则得到任意阶的逐步熵族；理论证明阶数>1与每步最可能token绑定（故追踪对数概率），1/2阶累积每步搜索努力的对数；并将SRE用于选择性预测与自适应计算（8粒子探针+两遍策略）。

Result: 1/2阶SRE在13个设置中10个为最佳零成本选择性预测信号；MATH500上四个模型及长度四分位内AUROC较对数概率高0.022–0.044，且留一设置时仍选中同阶；8粒子探针可识别受益于更多粒子的题目，两遍策略在MATH500上以少34.2%粒子（三种子）达到64粒子精度。

Conclusion: SMC解码可零成本提供有保证的序列级Rényi熵读出，构成优于对数概率的选择性预测信号，并能驱动高效自适应粒子分配。

Abstract: Quantifying uncertainty over an entire LLM completion is critical for selective prediction and adaptive compute. Zero-cost signals such as mean token entropy and log-probability are local to tokens or to one completion, and self-consistency needs many extra completions. We show that Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) decoding for power sampling already estimates the normalizing constant of the power distribution, which gives the sequence-level R'enyi entropy (SRE) of the model's completion distribution at zero marginal cost, with a guarantee at every particle budget. A R'enyi chain rule extends this readout to a family of stepwise entropies of any order. We prove that orders above one are tied to the most likely token of each step, which explains why they track log-probability, while the order-1/2 member accumulates the log of per-step search effort. This member is the best zero-cost selective-prediction signal in 10 of 13 settings. On MATH500 its AUROC is 0.022 to 0.044 above log-probability with all four models and within length quartiles, and the same order is selected when each setting is held out. An 8-particle probe identifies the problems that benefit from more particles, and a two-pass policy matches 64-particle accuracy on MATH500 with 34.2% fewer particles over three seeds.

</details>


### [173] [You Don't Need Feature Learning to Do In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13431)
*Joseph Turnbull,Jamie Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在核特征基上分解ICL线性回归任务，作者给出谱视角：样本量增大时，旋转不变核机依次学习记忆解的更高阶分量，泛化解只是记忆解的低阶截断，因此模型先泛化、随后退化为记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清ICL中"记忆"与"泛化"的关系及二者何时发生，并检验特征学习是否为ICL的必要条件。

Method: 研究旋转不变核机在Raventos等(2023)提出的ICL线性回归任务上的学习过程，将任务在核特征基（谱）中分解，刻画各阶分量随样本量的学习动力学；并与实证训练的Transformer对照。

Result: 随样本量增长，模型依次学习记忆解的高阶分量，这些分量依赖训练任务集的更高阶统计量；泛化解近似为记忆解的低阶截断，故训练过程中模型先泛化、后退化为记忆。实证Transformer确实通过核理论预测的高阶任务统计量记忆任务。

Conclusion: 三点结论：(1) 在ICL任务上学习"泛化解"实质上只是对记忆解欠拟合；(2) 核机能在此任务上泛化，说明特征学习对ICL并非必需；(3) 该核理论或可推广到具备特征学习能力的模型。

Abstract: How should we understand memorization vs. generalization in in-context learning (ICL) tasks, and when does one happen vs. the other? To investigate, we study how rotation-invariant kernel machines learn the ICL linear regression task proposed by Raventos et al. (2023). By decomposing the task in the kernel eigenbasis, we obtain a spectral picture of learning in this ICL setting. This picture reveals that as sample size grows, kernel machines learn successively-higher-degree components of the memorizing solution, which depend upon successively-higher-degree statistics of the training task set. The generalizing solution is approximately a low-degree truncation of the memorizing solution, and so the model first generalizes, then degrades into memorization as training proceeds.
  The takeaways are threefold. First, if this case is indicative, learning a "generalizing" solution on an ICL task is really just underfitting the memorizing solution! Second, the fact that kernel machines can generalize on this task means that feature learning is inessential to ICL. Third, transformers empirically trained to do ICL are shown to memorize tasks through the higher-order statistics of the training tasks that the kernel-based theory predicts, suggesting it applies even to models capable of feature learning.

</details>


### [174] [Fermat ProbCover: Coverage-Based Active Learning on Fermat Distance Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13448)
*Gokul Bhusal,Delanna Do,Kevin Miller,Ekaterina Merkurjev,Stephan Wojtowytsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PC-Fermat：用数据自适应的Fermat距离替代欧氏距离定义覆盖邻域，并结合加权欧氏邻域图与截断最短路算法高效计算Fermat覆盖图，在低预算主动学习场景中多数数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低预算下基于不确定性的主动学习方法易失效，基于覆盖的方法（如ProbCover）更有效，但其依赖欧氏距离，难以刻画数据的潜在几何结构与分布。

Method: 以Fermat距离（反映数据分布的数据自适应距离）替换欧氏覆盖邻域；构建加权欧氏邻域图，用截断最短路算法计算Fermat覆盖图；并给出该构造与完全图上Fermat覆盖结果等价的充分条件。

Result: 在8个分类数据集、不同标注预算（尤其极低预算）下评测，PC-Fermat在多数数据集上优于若干现有主动学习方法。

Conclusion: 用数据自适应的Fermat距离定义覆盖可提升覆盖式主动学习的样本选择质量，且截断图构造在给定充分条件下可精确保持完全图上的覆盖计算，兼顾效果与可计算性。

Abstract: Active learning aims to reduce annotation cost by selecting the most informative examples for labeling. In the low-budget regime, where uncertainty-based methods often fail, coverage-based active learning methods such as ProbCover have been shown to be effective. However, these methods typically rely on Euclidean distance, which may not adequately capture the underlying geometry of data. In this article, we propose PC-Fermat, a coverage-based active learning method that replaces Euclidean coverage neighborhoods with neighborhoods defined by a Fermat distance, i.e., a data-adaptive distance that reflects the underlying distribution of the data points. To facilitate computation, we construct a weighted Euclidean neighborhood graph and use a truncated shortest-path algorithm to compute the Fermat coverage graph. We also establish sufficient conditions under which this construction exactly preserves the Fermat coverage computed on the complete graph. Finally, we evaluate PC-Fermat on eight classification datasets across varying labeling budgets, with particular emphasis on the very low-budget regime. Our experimental results demonstrate that PC-Fermat outperforms several existing active learning methods on the majority of the datasets.

</details>


### [175] [SLIC: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning Small LMs for Multi-Turn Analog Circuit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13452)
*Yan Xu,Zhengqi Gao,Ching-Yun Ko,Zhang-Wei Hong,Wenjie Lu,Tao Yu,Xin Zhang,Duane S. Boning,Ruonan Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SLIC框架，用强化微调训练4B小模型实现闭环模拟电路优化，本地可部署且性能超越763B大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟IC设计需多轮仿真迭代与耦合权衡推理，但专有PDK使托管式大模型工作流难以实际部署；需一个本地化、可闭环优化的方案。

Method: SLIC=结构化电路表示+离散相对更新动作+领域/成功率门控多目标奖励+奖励引导的Rollout引导(RGRS，将每步仿真反馈转为策略学习与在线rollout构建信号)；基于开源65nm CMOS PTM训练4B模型。另发布含41种放大器拓扑的目标条件基准及ID验证、留出拓扑评估、跨工艺节点迁移协议。

Result: SLIC-Random八轮内ID四项规格Pass@1达87.1%，超DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash Think 48.5个百分点；SLIC-Softmax留出拓扑Pass@1 72.6%；跨5个未见节点零微调迁移；商用TSMC 65nm PDK上留出拓扑Pass@1 49.9%；32轮预算下留出拓扑Pass@1达83.2%。

Conclusion: 小规模本地模型经强化微调可在模拟电路闭环优化中兼具强性能与数据隐私可行性，性能随测试时仿真交互扩展。

Abstract: Analog integrated circuit (IC) design is a multi-turn, simulator-grounded engineering task that demands iterative reasoning over coupled performance tradeoffs, yet proprietary process design kits (PDKs) make hosted large language model workflows difficult to deploy in practical design settings. We present SLIC (Small LM Iterating on Circuits), a reinforcement fine-tuning framework that trains a 4B-scale, locally deployable language model for closed-loop analog circuit optimization. SLIC pairs structured circuit representations with discretized relative-update actions, domain- and success-gated multiobjective rewards, and Reward-Guided Rollout Steering (RGRS), which turns per-step simulator feedback into signals for both policy learning and online rollout construction in this sparse, highly constrained domain. We further release an open, goal-conditioned benchmark of 41 amplifier topologies with standardized protocols for in-distribution validation, held-out topology evaluation, and cross-technology-node transfer. Trained solely with open source 65 nm CMOS predictive technology models (PTM), SLIC-Random achieves 87.1% in-distribution four-specification Pass@1 within eight turns, surpassing the 763B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash Think by 48.5 percentage points. Beyond the training distribution, SLIC-Softmax reaches 72.6% Pass@1 on held-out topologies, and the trained policies consistently transfer across five unseen technology nodes without additional fine-tuning. This transfer extends to a commercial TSMC 65 nm CMOS PDK, where SLIC-Random achieves 49.9% held-out-topology Pass@1, demonstrating practical deployment through local inference without transmitting proprietary data to external LLM services. Performance further scales with test-time simulator interactions, reaching up to 83.2% held-out-topology Pass@1 at a 32-turn budget.

</details>


### [176] [Deep Personalization: Continuous Context Observation for Local Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13457)
*Yijia Shao,Song Bian,Junxian Guo,Jindong Jiang,Zhen Dong,Huizi Mao,Diyi Yang,Song Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"深度个性化"(deep personalization)闭环框架,通过独立Observer Agent持续观察用户活动、选择性保留长期上下文、从隐式反馈中学习干预时机,并在Coco桌面主动代理上实现端侧部署;OSWorld-Persona基准上宏F1从0.12-0.19提升至0.35-0.48,一周用户研究中F1从0.199升至0.515。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户往往难以判断何时以及如何向AI代理求助,导致大量潜在支持未被利用;主动代理虽有潜力弥补这一缺口,但有效主动性依赖对用户实时活动的理解与干预时机的个性化,否则容易造成干扰。现有方法缺乏对长时上下文的持续建模与从隐式反馈中自适应调整干预决策的闭环机制。

Method: 提出深度个性化闭环:观察—选择保留长期上下文—基于隐式反馈持续自适应。在Coco桌面主动代理中引入独立Observer Agent,监控桌面活动、维护紧凑的长期用户模型上下文,并利用参与、驳回、显式请求、重复工作模式等隐式信号学习何时干预;设计高效端侧策略使Observer Agent可在本地算力平台运行,原始活动数据不出设备。为可扩展评测,构建数据合成流水线,将现有计算机使用轨迹转化为带persona条件干预标签的OSWorld-Persona基准。

Result: 在OSWorld-Persona上,三个模型骨干的宏F1由直接提示的0.12-0.19提升至自适应后的0.35-0.48,相对增益2.4-3.0倍;为期一周的用户研究中,Coco的宏平均F1从0.199提升至0.515。

Conclusion: 端侧深度个性化可使主动代理的干预更及时、更贴合每个用户的日常工作,同时通过本地计算保障隐私。未来可进一步扩展长期用户建模与反馈信号维度,并在更长期、更广泛人群与任务中验证其泛化性与持续学习能力。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable, users must still recognize when and how to ask for help, leaving many opportunities for useful support unrealized. Proactive agents hold the promise to close this gap, but effective proactivity requires understanding users' real-time activities and personalizing intervention timing so that assistance is offered when it is likely to be helpful rather than disruptive. We introduce deep personalization, a closed-loop process that connects continuous context observation with continual agent adaptation: an agent observes user activity, selectively retains long-term context, and adapts intervention decisions from implicit feedback. We initiate deep personalization in Coco, a proactive desktop agent, by having a separate Observer Agent that monitors desktop activity, maintains compact long-term user modeling context, and learns when assistance is desirable from implicit signals such as engagement, dismissal, explicit requests, and repeated work patterns. We further develop efficient on-device strategies to enable the Observer Agent to run on local AI compute platforms without streaming raw activity data off-device. For evaluation at scale without collecting sensitive real-user activity, we introduce a data-synthesis pipeline that transforms existing computer-use trajectories into OSWorld-Persona, a benchmark with persona-conditioned intervention labels. On OSWorld-Persona, deep personalization improves macro F1 across three model backbones from 0.12-0.19 under direct prompting to 0.35-0.48 after adaptation, a 2.4-3.0x relative gain. In a week-long user study, we observe that Coco improves its macro-averaged F1 score from 0.199 to 0.515. These results suggest that on-device deep personalization can make proactive agents more timely and increasingly aligned with each user's everyday work.

</details>


### [177] [Leveraging Psychophysical Attentional Distribution for Gaze-Augmented Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13477)
*Chaeho Lee,Junyup Kim,Wansoo Kim,Young Min Jung,Sang Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将眼动数据构建为以注视点为中心的空间梯度注意力分布，并引入RLHF奖励模型训练，提升偏好预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF是偏好对齐的主流框架，近期工作把眼动（ET）数据作为额外监督信号，使奖励学习扎根于人类阅读行为；但以往模型多以离散注视点（fixation-centric）为单位，未充分利用认知心理学中关于人类字母识别的成熟结论（视觉注意具有空间梯度衰减特性）。

Method: 在既有注视点建模基础上，将视觉注意建模为以当前注视位置为中心的空间梯度（spatially graded）分布，并把该分布表示作为监督信号注入奖励模型训练，替代或补充纯注视点标签。

Result: 所提梯度注视分布训练的奖励模型在偏好预测准确率上优于基线模型；部分实验结果在定性上与阅读中人类注意的既有认知发现一致，提示模型可能捕捉到注意分配的认知有意义成分。

Conclusion: 以认知心理学启发的连续空间注意分布替代离散注视点，是一种有效且具认知可解释性的ET监督形式；未来可进一步验证其与人类阅读行为的定量一致性及在偏好对齐中的泛化性。

Abstract: Integrating human feedback into language models has gained increasing attention as a means to align model outputs with human preferences, with Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) serving as a prominent framework for preference-based alignment. Recent studies have incorporated eye-tracking (ET) data as an additional supervisory signal for reward model training, grounding reward learning in human reading behavior. Building on this approach, we extend prior fixation-centric models by leveraging established findings from cognitive psychology on human letter recognition. Specifically, we model visual attention as a spatially graded distribution centered on the current gaze location and incorporate this representation into reward model training. Our results show that the reward model trained with the proposed gaze distribution achieves higher preference prediction accuracy than a baseline model. Moreover, some results from our experiments are qualitatively consistent with established findings on human attention during reading, suggesting that they potentially capture cognitively meaningful aspects of human attention allocation.

</details>


### [178] [Sharp Sample Complexity of KL-Regularized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13480)
*Andrew Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 KL 正则化（最大熵/RLHF）目标的"控制即推断"后验学习复杂度，给出插件估计器的样本复杂度上界与匹配下界，并刻画其精确渐近常数与最优采样分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL 正则化 RL 的目标等价于图模型中的变分推断，其最优策略是轨迹后验，次优性等于 β 倍路径测度 KL 散度；但即便有生成模型，学习该后验所需样本数未知。已有 1/ε 保证在有效时域 H 上高达 H^5，且 MDP 的下界均不依赖温度 β。

Method: 针对有 K 个状态-动作对的折扣 MDP，分析插件式（plug-in）估计器的样本复杂度；建立与采样方式无关（含自适应采样）的下界；推导插件估计器的精确渐近常数（软 Q 函数估计的 Fisher 加权方差），并给出等频采样下的局部渐近极小极大性及自适应设计下的最优样本分配。

Result: 插件估计器以 Õ(KH^3/(ε max{ε,β}) + KH) 样本找到从每个初始状态出发 ε-最优的策略；当 ε ≲ min{H, H^2/β} 时该样本量不可改进；当 ε<β 时为 1/ε 速率，时域依赖与非正则化 RL 相同；小规模 MDP 仿真验证了速率与常数。

Conclusion: KL 正则化 RL 后验学习的最优样本复杂度被精确刻画：上界与下界匹配，温度 β 首次进入下界，且 ε<β 时正则化不增加时域代价；插件估计器在等频采样类中局部渐近极小极大，自适应采样可进一步优化分配。

Abstract: KL-regularized reinforcement learning, the objective behind maximum-entropy RL and RL from human feedback, is variational inference in the graphical model of "control as inference": the regularized optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories, and the suboptimality of a policy is $β$ times a KL divergence between path measures. However, it is not known how many samples it takes to learn this posterior, even with a generative model. Earlier $1/ε$ guarantees scale with $H^5$ in the effective horizon $H$, and no lower bound for MDPs depends on the temperature $β$. In this work, we show that for a discounted MDP with $K$ state-action pairs, the plug-in estimator finds a policy that is $ε$-optimal from every initial state with $\widetilde{O}(KH^3/(ε\max\{ε,β\}) + KH)$ samples, and that no algorithm, even one that chooses where to sample, can do with fewer when $ε\lesssim \min\{H, H^2/β\}$. For $ε< β$ this is a $1/ε$ rate with the same horizon dependence as unregularized RL. We also find the exact asymptotic constant of the plug-in estimator, a Fisher-weighted variance of the estimated soft Q-function. The estimator is locally asymptotically minimax among methods that sample every pair equally often, and we give the allocation of samples that is optimal among adaptive designs. Simulations on small MDPs match the rates and the constants.

</details>


### [179] [Evaluating Compositional Generalization in Transformers: The Role of Composition Equivalence and Module Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13498)
*Purva Pruthi,Andrew Yuan,Alexander D'Amour,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了transformer在组合泛化上的表现，指出以往评测结论不一致源于训练-测试划分的随意性，并识别出"组合等价"与"模块覆盖"两个关键分布因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合泛化（在部分模块组合上训练、泛化到未见组合）是分布外泛化的重要形式。已有实证工作结论不一致，因为评测往往只在有限的、临时设定的train-test划分上进行，缺乏系统性，无法揭示影响性能的真实因素。

Method: 开展系统性评测，将train-test分布解构为两个维度：(1)组合等价(composition equivalence)，即不同模块序列归约为相同端到端函数；(2)模块覆盖(module coverage)，即模块在训练与测试中出现于不同上下文。对比仅用最终输出的direct模型与使用中间及最终输出的step-by-step模型；并以Llama3-8B在LiveCodeBench代码输出预测任务上作案例研究。

Result: direct模型的表面性能主要来自利用组合等价（不同模块序列归约为相同端到端函数）；当基准消除这些等价后性能显著下降，说明其无法泛化到由已知模块构成、但产生新颖端到端函数的组合。性能随模块覆盖偏移增大而单调下降，direct与step-by-step模型均受影响。

Conclusion: 基准设计应避免包含组合等价以免高估direct模型的泛化能力；同时包含模块覆盖违规与组合等价的基准会加剧捷径学习。Llama3-8B在LiveCodeBench上的案例进一步印证组合等价对泛化性能的作用。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is an important form of out-of-distribution generalization, defined as the ability to train on some combinations of modules (e.g., functions such as sort, filter) and then generalize to unseen combinations. Despite substantial empirical work evaluating compositional generalization in transformers, findings have been inconsistent because evaluations were often conducted on a limited set of train-test splits, defined in an ad hoc manner. Through a systematic evaluation, we identify two key aspects of the train-test distribution that strongly affect performance: composition equivalence and module coverage. We show that the apparent performance of direct models (trained only on final outputs) results primarily from exploiting composition equivalences, meaning different sequences of modules that reduce to identical end-to-end functions. When benchmarks eliminate these equivalences, performance drops significantly, showing their inability to generalize to compositions of known modules that produce novel end-to-end functions. We further demonstrate that performance degrades monotonically as module coverage shifts increase (when modules appear in different contexts between train and test), affecting both direct and step-by-step models (trained on intermediate and final outputs). These findings have concrete implications for benchmark design: benchmarks that include compositional equivalences can lead to significant overestimation of the generalization abilities of direct models, and benchmarks that include both module coverage violations and compositional equivalences can exacerbate shortcut learning. As a case study, we demonstrate the role of compositional equivalence on the generalization performance of the pre-trained model Llama3-8B on the code-output prediction task in the coding benchmark LiveCodeBench.

</details>


### [180] [The RSI Tax: When Recursive Self-Improvement Is Not Worth the Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13524)
*Yitong Zhang,Ang Li,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"RSI Tax"（自改进过程的token成本），在持续任务流上做端到端评测，发现简单有界笔记基线在精度持平或更优的同时节省65.6%–71.0%总token；其中验证环节占RSI Tax的74.8%–93.6%，且大量成本花在后续未被使用的状态/产物上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）用智能体自身任务经验来提升能力，但其实际价值取决于性能增益能否抵偿自改进与任务执行的全部成本；现有工作多只报告精度提升，忽视了改进过程本身的开销，缺少端到端、含成本的公平评估。

Method: 在持续任务流上评测，每个任务在做后续更新前先评分（避免信息泄漏）；定义RSI Tax为自改进过程的token成本，与任务执行成本一并计入端到端评估；对比6种方法与2个语言模型；将RSI Tax分解为验证、探索、撰写三类成本，并追踪相应状态/产物在后续任务流中的使用情况。

Result: 有界笔记基线在观测精度上达到或超过Meta-Harness、EvoSkill、RRSI，平均总token节省65.6%–71.0%；三种RSI方法的验证成本占RSI Tax的74.8%–93.6%；相当比例的成本对应后续未被使用的状态或产物。

Conclusion: 昂贵RSI流程存在实际局限：部署智能体的性能改进未必能抵偿改进过程的开销。作者主张RSI目标应联合优化RSI Tax与期望任务执行成本，同时保证智能体能力在任务流上持续增长。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) seeks to improve an agent using its own task experience. Its practical value depends on whether performance gains justify the full cost of self-improvement and task execution. We evaluate this trade-off on continual task streams, scoring each task before its feedback is used for subsequent updates. We define the \emph{RSI Tax} as the token cost of the self-improvement process and include it alongside task-execution costs in end-to-end evaluation. Across six methods and two language models, a bounded-note baseline matches or exceeds the observed accuracy of Meta-Harness, EvoSkill, and RRSI, with mean total-token savings of 65.6-71.0\%. We decompose the RSI Tax into verification, exploration, and writing costs, and track subsequent use of the associated state or artifact. For these three RSI methods, verification accounts for 74.8--93.6% of the aggregate RSI Tax. A substantial share of the RSI Tax is also associated with states or artifacts that are not used later in the evaluated streams. These results expose a practical limitation of costly RSI procedures: improvements in the deployed agent need not justify the expense of the improvement process. They motivate RSI objectives that jointly optimize the RSI Tax and expected task-execution cost while ensuring continued growth in agent capabilities across the task stream.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond the Log-Linear Frontier: Pre-Asymptotic Analysis of Finite-Budget Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13534)
*Tianyu Zhao,Zhixiang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从谱异质性与域异质性出发，用Mellin–Barnes协方差预解式分析与KKT对偶刻画经典神经缩放律在有限预算下的偏离，给出AoN初始化的闭式解、跨域采样课程与等算力分配漂移的解析结论，并在45个算子谱、Pythia、Chinchilla、RegMix上取得显著降误差与降regret效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典缩放律把超额风险建模为模型容量N与数据量D的可分离幂律，但在有限预算下经验学习曲线与算力最优分配会偏离该对数线性前沿，本文旨在解释并建模这种有限尺度偏离的成因。

Method: 容量轴：对协方差预解式做Mellin–Barnes分析，得到谱模式与正则化模式竞争的三项展开，刻画有限尺度曲率与对数转变；因窄试点网格上多幂律拟合病态，用一阶Aitken差分消去损失下界以获得良态闭式初始化。数据轴：用KKT对偶把K域混合化为标量注水问题，得到有限预算瓶颈界、在线采样课程与用于跨域迁移的单纯形Hessian度量。等算力前沿C=6ND上证明最优分配指数漂移正比于容量与数据指数方差，并用Schur补约简分离内在漂移与冻结超参偏差。

Result: 在45个算子谱、Pythia（70M–12B）、Chinchilla（245次运行）与RegMix（17域）上，估计器达到5.89%留出MAPE，高算力分配regret最多降低76.4%，跨尺度混合RMSE降低59.4%且选择regret为零。

Conclusion: 有限尺度偏离可由谱异质性与域异质性解析解释；结合Aitken差分的良态估计、注水式域混合与指数漂移理论，可在实际大规模训练中显著提升拟合精度与算力分配效率。

Abstract: Classical neural scaling laws model excess risk as a separable power law in model capacity $N$ and dataset size $D$. At finite budgets, however, empirical learning curves and compute-optimal allocations depart from this log-linear frontier. This paper analyzes how spectral and domain heterogeneity govern these finite-scale departures. Along the capacity axis, Mellin--Barnes analysis of covariance resolvents yields a three-term expansion where competing spectral and regularization modes produce finite-scale curvature and logarithmic transitions. Because fitting multiple power laws on narrow pilot grids is ill-conditioned, we eliminate the loss floor via first-order Aitken differencing to obtain a well-conditioned closed-form initializer. Along the data axis, KKT duality reduces $K$-domain mixing to scalar water-filling, yielding finite-budget bottleneck bounds, an online sampling curriculum, and a simplex Hessian metric for cross-domain transfer. Along the iso-compute frontier $C=6ND$, we prove that the optimal allocation exponent drifts in proportion to capacity and data exponent variances, while Schur-complement reduction separates intrinsic drift from frozen-hyperparameter bias. Across 45 operator spectra, Pythia (70M--12B), Chinchilla (245 runs), and RegMix (17 domains), our estimators achieve $5.89\%$ holdout MAPE, cut high-compute allocation regret by up to $76.4\%$, and reduce cross-scale mixture RMSE by $59.4\%$ with zero selection regret.

</details>


### [182] [Adaptive catalyst discovery emerges from composable scientific intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13536)
*Wentao Li,Qilong Cai,Qi Lei,Jinxing Chen,Ruixuan Chen,Jiangjie Qiu,Botian Wang,Kai Zhao,Honghao Chen,Leyi Zhao,Zemeng Wang,Yijun Li,Jun Yin,Fangfang Li,Hongxuan Liu,Yingdong Lu,Yawen Ouyang,Manu Suvarna,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Kostya S. Novoselov,Hao Zhou,Jiong Lu,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可组合催化智能框架CatAI，通过约180万条结构—性质关系训练30个物理化学编码器，作为可复用的中间层支撑下游任务模型，在基准任务上预测误差降低7.8%，并经实验验证实现高活性催化剂发现与闭环优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 催化是化学制造的基础，但催化剂设计知识分散于实验观测、计算数据和各催化子学科之间，缺乏可组合、可复用的统一表征，难以跨反应与跨机理迁移。

Method: 构建物理启发的模块化AI框架CatAI：利用180万条结构—性质关系学习，并组织为30个物理化学编码器构成可复用中间层；基于该层构建面向特定反应与目标的任务模型，结合计算筛选与模型—实验迭代反馈进行催化剂发现与自主闭环优化。

Result: 基准任务上任务平均预测误差较次优模型低7.8%；发现可通行受限口袋的新型催化剂，在工业均相聚合中活性为商业基准的9倍；指导Cu基双原子（geminal-atom）光催化剂的自主闭环优化，用于C–O偶联反应并超过传统Ni基体系。

Conclusion: CatAI将性质层面数据与催化目标相连接，通过计算筛选与迭代实验反馈，支持物理化学知识在机理不同体系间的复用，为可组合的催化剂智能设计提供范式。

Abstract: Catalysis underpins chemical manufacturing, yet knowledge for catalyst design remains dispersed across experimental observations, computational data and catalysis subdisciplines. To overcome this challenge, we present Composable Catalytic Intelligence (CatAI), a physics-grounded modular AI framework that learns 1.8 million structure-property relations and organizes them into 30 physicochemical encoders. This reusable intermediate layer enables task-specific models to guide catalyst discovery for targeted reactions and objectives. Across benchmark tasks, CatAI achieves a 7.8% lower task-averaged prediction error than the next-best models. In experimental validation, CatAI identified a new confined-passable pocket catalyst applicable in industrial homogeneous polymer synthesis with 9 times the activity of the commercial benchmark. In parallel, it guided autonomous closed-loop optimization of a highly active Cu-based geminal-atom photocatalyst for C-O coupling reactions surpassing the traditional Ni-based systems. This approach connects property-level data to catalytic objectives, supporting physicochemical knowledge reuse across mechanistically distinct systems through computational screening and iterative model-experiment feedback.

</details>


### [183] [Sanity Checks for Attention Surgery: Null Models and Matched Interventions for Representation-Motivated Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13543)
*Basavaraj R M,Aryan Bharat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出三项可复用检验（噪声地板、几何零假设、方向特异性）来评估"统计量→删除→改进"式Transformer改造，并以自注意力移除为例，在9个预训练模型上证明head-value对齐大部分可由因果零假设解释，且训练收益依赖自身value方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构改进常以"某内部统计量较大→据此删除该成分→报告性能提升"为范式，但该推理链条缺乏对统计量显著性、几何基线与方向必要性的严格检验，易将结构性伪影误判为冗余。

Method: 提出三项检验（噪声地板、几何零假设、方向特异性），应用于"自注意力移除"（去除注意力头输出中与其自身value向量对齐的分量）。分析9个多头预训练模型共5408个头（124M–6.9B参数）；构建更早位置因果零模型；在GQA下比较组内/组间对齐；用折半信度评估per-head干预效应与结构统计量的可分辨性；并进行预注册3臂×8种子配对因子实验（每run 4.0e8 tokens，共399,900,672 tokens）。

Result: 因果零假设解释48%–63%的head-value余弦对齐且无单调尺度趋势；GQA下对齐具组内特异性（组内+0.24~+0.27，组间-0.11~-0.19）；干预效应折半信度GPT-2 r_Δ=0.80、Pythia-160M 0.42、Pythia-410M 0.53，而结构自余弦信度>0.99；预注册实验：固定任意rank-one方向相对基线+0.0011 nats（p=0.197，无差异），自方向移除-0.0029 nats（Holm校正p=0.0023），并优于固定方向控制-0.0040 nats（p=0.0018）。

Conclusion: 在50.9M规模下训练收益需要自身value方向而非任意固定方向；但冻结模型中较大的余弦对齐本身不足以证明冗余，需综合零假设基线与干预信度判断；"哪个统计量能预测其自身干预"具有模型依赖性。

Abstract: Transformer architectural modifications frequently measure a statistic on internal representations, observe that it is large, remove it architecturally, and report an improvement. Interpreting that sequence requires evaluating whether the statistic exceeds its noise floor, whether it survives a geometric null, and whether the training gain requires the specific direction removed. We formulate these requirements as three reusable checks and apply them to self-attention removal, which deletes the component of an attention head's output aligned with its own value vector. Across nine multi-head pretrained models (5,408 heads, 124M to 6.9B parameters), an earlier-position causal null accounts for 48% to 63% of the head-value cosine alignment, with no monotone scale trend. Under grouped-query attention, this alignment is specific to each head's own key-value group and reverses across groups (+0.24 to +0.27 within-group, -0.11 to -0.19 across-group). Split-half reliability demonstrates that the per-head intervention effect is resolvable in GPT-2 ($r_Δ= 0.80$) but attenuated in Pythia-160M (0.42) and Pythia-410M (0.53), whereas structural self-cosine reliability is near-perfect in all three ($r_{\text{stat}} > 0.99$); which statistic predicts its own intervention is model-dependent. In a pre-registered 3-arm by 8-seed paired factorial at $4.0 \times 10^8$ tokens per run (399,900,672 tokens), a fixed arbitrary rank-one direction does not differ from baseline (+0.0011 nats, $p = 0.197$), while self-directed removal lowers validation loss (-0.0029 nats, Holm-corrected $p = 0.0023$) and outperforms the fixed arbitrary control directly (-0.0040 nats, $p = 0.0018$). At 50.9M scale the training benefit requires the own-value direction rather than a fixed arbitrary one, but a large cosine alignment alone does not establish redundancy in frozen models.

</details>


### [184] [Closing the BF16-FP16 Convergence Speed Gap in RL Post-Training with Stochastic Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13545)
*Mahdi Nazemi,Oleg Rybakov,Tijmen Blankevoort,Asit Mishra,Carlo del Mundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有BF16混合精度强化学习（RLVR）训练收敛慢于FP16，根因是权重粘滞；提出在FP32影子权重量化到BF16时使用随机舍入（stochastic rounding），在不同模型与数据集上达到FP16的收敛速度，同时KL散度低于大学习率BF16方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BF16因动态范围与FP32相同、收敛行为接近而被广泛用于LLM预训练。但在可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中学习率通常较低，此时标准混合精度BF16训练在相同学习率下收敛比FP16慢。作者将此差距归因于"权重粘滞"：FP32影子权重用就近舍入（round-to-nearest）量化到BF16时，微小更新常无法改变用于rollout生成与训练的BF16权重，需经多个优化器步才能体现，导致收敛迟缓。

Method: 分析权重粘滞现象并验证：提高BF16学习率可缓解粘滞、匹配FP16收敛速度，但会增大相对参考模型的KL散度。因此改为在FP32影子权重→BF16量化时采用随机舍入（stochastic rounding），使微小更新以概率方式反映到BF16权重中。

Result: 随机舍入在不同模型与数据集上均匹配FP16的收敛速度，且KL散度低于采用更大学习率的BF16训练。同时保留BF16更宽的动态范围，无需loss scaling（不同于FP16）。

Conclusion: 随机舍入是一种简单有效的量化策略，可在低学习率的RLVR场景中兼顾BF16的训练效率（收敛速度）与稳定性（KL散度），并规避FP16的loss scaling需求，为BF16混合精度RL训练提供更优默认选择。

Abstract: BF16 is widely used to pretrain large language models because it has the same dynamic range as FP32 and converges similarly to FP32 training. However, in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where learning rates are typically low, we find that standard mixed-precision BF16 training converges more slowly than FP16 training at the same learning rate. We attribute this gap to weight sticking: when FP32 shadow weights are quantized to BF16 using round-to-nearest, small updates often leave the BF16 weights used for rollout generation and training unchanged. Consequently, several optimizer steps may be needed before updates to the shadow weights are reflected in the BF16 weights. Raising the BF16 learning rate reduces weight sticking and matches FP16's convergence speed, but it also increases the KL divergence from the reference model. We instead apply stochastic rounding when quantizing the FP32 shadow weights to BF16. This approach matches FP16's convergence speed across different models and datasets while reducing the KL divergence relative to BF16 training with a larger learning rate. Unlike FP16, it keeps BF16's wider dynamic range and needs no loss scaling.

</details>


### [185] [Massive Activations Confound Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13548)
*Nathan Delisle,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器(SAE)常用的方差解释率与消融损伤评价会被"大规模激活"污染；作者通过去除PC1再训练、重建时加回PC1的修正方法，显著提升了特征的可复现性并改善了下游指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAE评价指标（variance explained 与 ablation damage）在存在大规模激活时失效：大规模激活使高达99.9%的激活方差集中在第一主成分(PC1)上，而PC1缺乏唯一的稀疏分解，导致不同训练运行从不同特征集重建它，进而使重要特征排名不稳定、方差解释率被虚高（宣称99.96%却仍有33%的非PC1方差未被解释）。因此需要一种修正方法来绕开这一问题。

Method: 训练SAE前先从激活中移除PC1分量，仅对剩余激活进行SAE训练，然后在校重阶段把PC1加回重建结果，从而绕过PC1无法被唯一稀疏分解的问题。

Result: 在受影响的模型中，top-50特征的跨运行共享率从4%–39.5%提升到73%–99.5%；在相同TopK稀疏度下，模型性能退化最多减少44%，内容重建误差最多降低33.9%。同时还揭示原评价的偏差：50个最重要特征中平均有47个在另一次运行中无法匹配（随机50个仅约27个）。

Conclusion: 大规模激活会系统性破坏SAE的方差解释与消融损伤评价，导致特征排名与解释率不可靠；显式剥离PC1的修正方法能大幅提升特征可复现性并改善下游重建与模型损伤指标，应作为受影响模型中SAE评估与训练的默认处理。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are commonly evaluated by variance explained and ablation damage. We show how massive activations undermine both evaluations, and propose a correction. Massive activations cause up to 99.9% of activation variance to lie along the first principal component (PC1). Because the PC1 component has no unique sparse decomposition, each SAE run rebuilds it from a different set of features. These features dominate importance rankings: an average of 47 of the 50 most important features are unmatched in a separate run, compared with about 27 of a random 50. A dictionary can also report 99.96% of variance explained while leaving 33% of the variance outside PC1 unexplained. We bypass PC1 by training the SAE on activations with PC1 removed, then adding PC1 to the reconstruction. In affected models, the top-50 feature sharing rate rises from 4-39.5% to 73-99.5%. At the same TopK sparsity, the resulting SAE degrades the model up to 44% less and reduces content reconstruction error by up to 33.9%.

</details>


### [186] [Tabular Foundation Models in Financial Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13550)
*Ed deHaan,Allen H. Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把表格基础模型（TFM）引入会计研究，在“预测企业下一年盈余变动”这一基准任务上评估其表现，发现 TabFM 精度优于微调大语言模型与树模型，且比大语言模型更快、算力需求更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会计与金融预测需要精确且易用的工具；TFM 能直接输出数值预测并具备上下文学习能力，且预训练于合成表格数据，可规避记忆企业真实结果所导致的“前视偏差”，因而具备作为新型预测工具的潜力，但在会计领域尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 技术性说明（technical note）。以预测企业下一年盈余变动为基准任务，将 Google Research 的 TabFM 与近期会计研究中微调的开放权重（open-weight）大语言模型以及若干树模型进行对比，考察精度与计算效率（速度、算力消耗）。

Result: TabFM 的预测精度高于所有对比模型；相比大语言模型，其运行更快、计算强度更低。部分树模型设定（specification）的预测速度快于 TabFM，因此两者之间的选择体现为精度与速度的权衡。

Conclusion: TFM 有望成为会计与金融预测的新工具；但评估仅基于单一基准任务，且不太可能有某一类模型在所有情境下均占优，结论具有任务局限性。

Abstract: This technical note introduces tabular foundation models (TFMs) to accounting research and evaluates their performance in one common benchmark task: predicting firms' next-year earnings changes. TFMs produce numeric predictions and learn in context, without post-release training or tuning, making them natural and easy to use for forecasting. The TFMs we study are pretrained on synthetic tabular data, so they avoid look-ahead bias from memorizing firms' outcomes. We compare Google Research's TabFM with a fine-tuned open-weight large language model developed in recent accounting research and with several tree-based models. TabFM is more accurate than all comparison models. It is also faster and less computationally intensive than the large language model. Several tree-based specifications produce predictions more quickly than TabFM, so choosing between them involves a trade-off between accuracy and speed. We conclude that TFMs have promise as a new tool for forecasting in accounting and finance. However, our evaluation is based on a single benchmark task, and no class of models is likely to dominate in all settings.

</details>


### [187] [Discovering Structurally Plausible and Interpretable Cognitive Models with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13561)
*Boyoung Kwon,Jiyoon Seo,Sang Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DisCo：一个由 LLM 引导、基于进化搜索的符号认知模型发现框架，通过 Validator 与 Cleaner 两个结构组件保证模型的结构合理性与参数可识别性，在三个行为任务上取得与任务专用基线相当的拟合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算认知建模依赖人工构建，耗时且需大量领域专业知识；近期基于 LLM 的自动化方法虽能生成模型，但往往以预测精度为优先，牺牲了结构合理性与参数可识别性。

Method: DisCo 采用 LLM 引导的进化搜索来发现可解释的符号认知模型，框架包含两个结构组件：Validator 用于过滤结构上无效的模型；Cleaner 通过删除冗余参数提升参数可识别性。

Result: 在三个行为任务上，DisCo 一致地发现性能可与任务专用基线相竞争（competitive）的模型。消融分析表明，Validator 对恢复具有心理学意义的结构至关重要；去除该组件后，模型倾向违反行为约束，与外部认知测量指标的关联也更不一致。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的模型发现若与恰当的结构约束相结合，可产出超越单纯预测拟合、具备认知合理性的模型。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling has traditionally relied on manual model construction, which is a time-intensive process that requires substantial domain expertise. Although recent LLM-based approaches have begun to automate model generation, they often prioritize predictive accuracy at the expense of structural plausibility and parameter identifiability. We introduce DisCo, an LLM-guided framework that discovers interpretable symbolic cognitive models through evolutionary search with two structural components: a Validator that filters structurally invalid models, and a Cleaner that promotes parameter identifiability by removing redundant parameters. Across three behavioral tasks, DisCo consistently discovers models that achieve performance competitive with task-specific baselines. Ablation analyses show that the Validator is important for recovering psychologically meaningful structure. Without this component, models tend to violate behavioral constraints and show less consistent associations with external cognitive measures. These results suggest that LLM-driven model discovery, when coupled with appropriate structural constraints, can yield cognitively plausible models that go beyond predictive fit.

</details>


### [188] [Investigating Composite Modeling in Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Scientific Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13565)
*Ricky Ye,Sahas Goli,Muhammad Ali,Poompol Buathong,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究复合建模（Composite modeling）在多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）中的收益及其驱动因素，发现当中间响应到最终目标的映射非线性且非双射时收益更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目标BO中，利用可观测中间响应可显著提升采样效率，但该优势在MOBO中尚未被充分研究，且收益大小受何因素影响不明。

Method: 先推导单目标条件下中间观测不提供额外预测信息（复合与黑箱方法共享同一warped GP先验）的理论条件；再在8个合成与应用启发的科学设计基准上，用四种MOBO方法进行对照数值实验，其中黑箱方法以标准多输出GP建模目标函数。

Result: 两类研究均表明：当中间响应到最终目标的映射为非双射且非仿射时，复合建模相对黑箱建模的优化收益更大。

Conclusion: 复合建模在MOBO中的增益与映射的结构性质密切相关，为实践中选择是否建模中间响应提供了判据。

Abstract: Scientific design often requires balancing competing objectives that are expensive to evaluate through experiments or simulations. Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) addresses this challenge by using probabilistic models to guide the selection of promising designs. In many applications, tunable inputs map to observable intermediate responses, which are then combined through known functions to compute the final objectives. Prior work on Bayesian optimization of composite functions shows that learning from these intermediate responses, which we call Composite modeling, can substantially improve sampling efficiency in single-objective optimization, compared to methods that condition only on black-box observations of the objective function. The benefits of Composite modeling in MOBO, however, remain understudied. In this work, we study the benefits of Composite modeling in MOBO and investigate the factors driving variation in its gains. We first establish single-objective conditions under which intermediate observations provide no additional predictive information beyond objective values, where composite and black box methods use the same warped Gaussian process prior. Controlled numerical experiments with versions of four MOBO methods across eight synthetic and application-inspired scientific-design benchmarks examine how these conditions relate to optimization gains in the multi-objective setting, where the black box approach models the objective functions with a standard multi-output Gaussian process. Both investigations support the claim that composite modeling tends to help more when the function mapping intermediate responses to final objectives is non-bijective and non-affine.

</details>


### [189] [Metamorphic testing of learned AC optimal power flow surrogates reveals representation dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13566)
*Stephen Peng,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文用13条蜕变关系审计17种AC-OPF代理模型/管线配置，发现每种配置至少违反6条关系，说明仅报告单一序列化精度不足以证明代理模型的可靠性，建议同时报告表示一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单一电网序列化上评估学习型AC-OPF代理模型，无法判断其精度是否依赖于该编码；需要检验模型对表示变换的依赖程度。

Method: 设计13条蜕变关系（每条规定变换的有效条件与输出比较方式），审计17种配置：2个已发布训练检查点与15个随机权重架构/机制对照；报告宽容度上下浮动10倍；对GridSFM-Open v1.1做源码检查与中间张量干预；另做case500训练实验考察数据增强，并排查GPU/CPU特征求解器不一致。

Result: 所有配置在更紧或更松的10倍报告宽容度下均至少违反6条关系。GridSFM-Open v1.1的问题源于一个索引bug及三项非规范/数值不稳定的预处理选择，修复后检查点尚未测试。case500增强实验中，4个模型里3个的变换视图误差下降，全部4个的中位差异与约束违反P95下降，但部分关系反而变差，且所有预测仍AC不可行；原始拉普拉斯基线的大幅提升主要来自GPU/CPU特征求解器不匹配。

Conclusion: 建议在精度、可行性与成本之外报告表示一致性；结果既不支持部署就绪，也不支持普遍不变性。

Abstract: Evaluating a learned AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) surrogate on one grid serialization cannot show whether its accuracy depends on that encoding. We test this dependence with thirteen metamorphic relations, each specifying when a transformation is valid and how to compare its outputs. Our audit covers 17 model and pipeline configurations: two released trained checkpoints and 15 random-weight architecture or mechanism controls. Every configuration violates at least six relations, even at reporting tolerances ten times tighter or looser. For GridSFM-Open v1.1, source inspection and intermediate-tensor interventions point to an indexing bug and three noncanonical or numerically unstable preprocessing choices; a repaired checkpoint remains untested. In a separate case500 training experiment, augmentation lowers transformed-view error in three of four models and median discrepancy and constraint-violation P95 in all four. These benefits depend on the training protocol: some relations worsen, and every evaluated prediction remains AC infeasible. A GPU/CPU eigensolver mismatch accounts for most of the large raw-Laplacian-baseline gain. We recommend reporting representation consistency alongside accuracy, feasibility, and cost. These results establish neither deployment readiness nor universal invariance.

</details>


### [190] [Market-Conditioned JEPA: Learning from Unpredictable Market Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13572)
*Yuqi Wang,Fengyuan Liu,Mukai Li,Haochen Luo,Lei Li,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出市场条件化JEPA（MC-JEPA），利用资产间未来嵌入构造的留一市场条件与因子载荷，将可预测的市场共同冲击从噪声中分离，在A股分钟级数据上于收益、暴露、风险与流动性多目标任务全面优于逐资产JEPA及强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列的很大变异来自无法由资产自身历史预测的市场共同冲击，而逐资产自监督学习（如逐资产JEPA）将其视为不可约噪声，导致资产特异成分的学习目标被市场噪声干扰，预测效率受限。

Method: 基于潜在因子模型的理论分析表明：以已实现市场因子为条件可使资产市场暴露成为最优预测的必要信息，并降低资产特异成分学习所面对的噪声。据此构建MC-JEPA联合嵌入预测架构：训练时为每个资产用其他资产的未来目标嵌入构造留一市场条件，并经由该资产自身表征导出的因子载荷注入预测器；推理后恢复归一化去除的波动尺度。

Result: 11年分钟级中国A股数据上，MC-JEPA在收益、市场暴露、风险、流动性九类目标上均优于逐资产JEPA，暴露/风险/流动性提升最强且统计显著；在所有流动性目标及2小时至5日收益期限上取得最优RankIC，优于时间序列基础模型与自监督基线，并在历史回测中取得最佳组合表现。消融显示收益与暴露增益依赖条件与已实现市场走势对齐；无标签条件下预测器因子载荷的市场beta排序接近有监督探针。合成因子模型实验验证条件化同时改善市场暴露与资产特异成分的估计。

Conclusion: 将市场因子作为条件而非噪声，可统一提升金融时序自监督表征在多任务上的质量，并为无标签的beta估计与组合构建提供有效途径。

Abstract: Much of the variation in financial time series is driven by market-wide shocks that cannot be predicted from an asset's own history. Per-asset self-supervised learning treats this variation as irreducible noise. Under a latent factor model, we show that conditioning prediction on the realized market factor makes the asset's market exposure necessary for optimal prediction, while reducing the noise against which the asset-specific component is learned. Building on this insight, we introduce Market-Conditioned JEPA (MC-JEPA), a joint-embedding predictive architecture for financial time series. During training, we construct a leave-one-out market condition for each asset from the future target embeddings of other assets and incorporate it through factor loadings derived from the asset's own representation. On 11 years of minute-level Chinese A-share data, MC-JEPA improves over per-asset JEPA across nine targets spanning returns, market exposure, risk, and liquidity, with the strongest and most statistically significant gains in exposure, risk, and liquidity. It achieves the best RankIC on all liquidity targets and on return horizons from two hours to five days, outperforming time-series foundation models and self-supervised baselines, and delivers the best portfolio performance in a historical backtest. Ablations show that the return and exposure gains require the condition to be aligned with the realized market movement. After restoring the volatility scale removed by normalization, the predictor's factor loadings rank stocks by market beta nearly as well as a supervised probe, without labels. A synthetic factor-model study with known parameters shows that conditioning improves the recovery of both market exposure and the asset-specific component.

</details>


### [191] [Unified Optimization of Traffic Signal and Lane Channelization for an Isolated Intersection Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13599)
*Jiwon Kang,Jiyoung Kim,Hyunseok Jun,Keemin Sohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习（DQN）的交叉口信号配时与车道渠化（可变车道标线）联合实时优化方法，并用人类反馈规则过滤危险动作切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号控制是实时动态问题，车道渠化设计是静态问题，二者联合优化困难；已有MILP联合模型在交通流模式变化时需重复求解，且规模增大后计算时间不可接受，因此需要一种可实时求解的鲁棒方法。

Method: 构建可同时输出信号相位与车道标线方案的联合动作DQN；为避免车道标线与信号相位不匹配的突变危及驾驶员安全，采用人类反馈训练模型，以规则方式过滤不可行动作转移；实施上需在每个停止线处配备可变车道标线指示标志。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法通过提高交叉口通行能力（throughput）改善了交叉口运行性能。

Conclusion: 强化学习可在实时层面替代重复求解MILP，实现信号与车道渠化的联合优化，并通过人类反馈约束保证动作切换的安全可行性。

Abstract: Optimizing traffic signal control has been a long-lasting critical issue in traffic engineering, and a number of robust methodologies have emerged to solve the problem so far. On the other hand, the road design for lane channelization is a static problem that is difficult to solve jointly with the real-time traffic signal control. If traffic volume does not vary, it is possible that both problems can be solved in an integrated manner as a single static optimization problem. Recently, researchers have devised a mixed integer linear problem (MILP) to jointly determine the traffic signal and road width designs. However, this joint optimization problem must be solved repeatedly whenever the traffic flow pattern varies. Solving the MILP may also take impractical computing time as the problem size gets larger. We present a robust methodology for solving the joint optimization problem using reinforcement learning (RL) on a real-time basis. Abrupt changes in lane markings with an incompatible signal phase can jeopardize drivers. To sidestep such a dangerous action change, we train a model with human feedback to filter infeasible action transitions in a rule-based manner. We devised a deep Q-network that can accommodate joint actions for traffic signal and lane marking plans. To ensure the implementation of the proposed operation, a variable sign for lane markings is necessary at each stop line of an intersection. The experimental results confirm that the proposed methodology enhances the intersection performance by increasing throughput.

</details>


### [192] [MouldIt: Data Efficiency of Multi-Task Temporal Representation Learning in Injection Moulding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13646)
*Abdullah Ayad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MouldIt——时序卷积与注意力机制结合的变分自编码器，用于注塑过程的多任务表征学习，在已知类分类与未知类检测上优于传统机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工业多任务解决方案需要理解底层工艺过程的模型，因此需要研究注塑过程表征学习，并考察其数据效率与多模态相对经典机器学习方法的优势。

Method: 基于Erlanger Träger零件的真实参考周期合成工艺感知数据集；提出MouldIt（时序卷积+注意力的变分自编码器），重建模内信号并将零件分类为合格或某一合成失效模式；在单模态与多模态、9种数据规模下评估已知类分类与未知类检测两任务，与accept-all基线及kNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、PCA（均改造以支持双任务）对比。

Result: 单模态下从4000周期起、多模态下从2000周期起，MouldIt在两任务上均超越经典方法；分拣损失分别为6.0±0.7%（单模态）与1.0±0.3%（多模态），在所有基线及PCA+随机森林组合中排名第一。

Conclusion: 基于深度表征学习的MouldIt在多任务注塑过程建模中具备更高的数据效率与多模态优势，显著优于经典机器学习方法，适合智能工业多任务场景。

Abstract: Smart industrial solutions that can address multiple tasks require models that understand the underlying processes. We examine representation learning for modeling the injection moulding process as an example of such solutions. We consider its data efficiency and multi-modal benefit compared to classical machine learning alternatives. To this end, we synthesize a process-informed dataset based on a real reference cycle of an Erlanger Träger part. We then introduce MouldIt, a temporal convolution and attention based variational autoencoder that reconstructs in-mould signals, while classifying parts as acceptable or as one of our synthetic failure modes. We evaluate MouldIt on two tasks, known-class classification and unknown-class detection, across nine dataset sizes in single-modal and multi-modal settings. We compare it with an accept-all baseline and the classical methods k-nearest neighbors, logistic regression, random forests, and principal component analysis, which we adapt to support both tasks. MouldIt outperforms classical methods across both tasks from 4000 cycles in the single-modal setting and from 2000 cycles in the multi-modal setting. It achieves part sorting losses of 6.0 $\pm$ 0.7% and 1.0 $\pm$ 0.3% in the single-modal and multi-modal settings respectively, ranking first across all baselines and a combination of principal component analysis and random forests.

</details>


### [193] [CP-MLPs: A Tensor-Rank Theory of Tied and Untied MLP Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13647)
*Md Rifat Arefin,Farzaneh Heidari,Irina Rish,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CP-MLPs张量框架，将FFN中每个乘性单元分解为检测/读取/写入的秩-1交互，证明tied与untied gated块存在精确的张量秩分离，说明"解绑"的宽度效率优势取决于目标函数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer前馈块混合了平方激活、门控、参数共享/不共享等多种设计，各自对表达能力的独立贡献尚不清楚，缺乏统一的理论刻画。

Method: 用CP（CANDECOMP/PARAFAC）张量分解刻画乘性单元：每个单元贡献一个rank-1交互，承载检测输入特征、读取值、写入输出三种角色。据此对比tied gated（如ReLU²，检测器与值共享方向）与untied gated（如ReGLU、SwiGLU，二者可不同），做逐次数的张量秩分析；并以border rank分析近似极限，配合宽度-损失实验与匹配输入投影预算的训练对比。

Result: 得到精确的度次张量秩分离：对非定二次交互与角色非对称单项式，untied单元宽度效率严格更高；而对对称纯幂目标，tied单元在结构上恰好匹配。实验发现损失平台出现在精确表示宽度之下，但平台本身不构成正的近似间隙——任意精度近似由border rank决定；训练后的门控MLP中，untied块在部分对比中于匹配输入投影预算下保持优势。

Conclusion: 解绑（untied）的宽度优势并非普遍成立，而取决于目标结构：非对称交互受益于解绑，对称纯幂则天然适配tied；理论上的表示间隙应以border rank而非宽度平台来判定。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward blocks combine several design choices: squared activations, gating, and tied versus untied parameter sharing, whose separate contributions to expressivity remain poorly understood. We introduce CP-MLPs, a tensor framework in which each multiplicative unit contributes a rank-1 interaction with three roles: detecting an input feature, reading a value, and writing to the output. Tied gated blocks such as ReLU$^2$ force the detector and value roles to share the same direction; untied gated blocks such as ReGLU and SwiGLU let them differ. We show that this distinction yields exact, degree-wise tensor-rank separations: untied detector-value units are strictly more width-efficient for indefinite quadratic interactions and role-asymmetric monomials, while tied units are structurally matched to symmetric pure-power targets. The width advantage of untying therefore depends on the target structure. Experiments show loss plateaus below the exact-representation width, but these plateaus alone do not establish a positive approximation gap: arbitrarily accurate approximation is governed by border rank. In trained gated MLPs, untied blocks retain an advantage in selected comparisons at matched input-projection budget. Code is available at https://github.com/rarefin/CP-MLPs.

</details>


### [194] [SepsisFlow: an event-stream foundation model for continuous risk warning and trajectory modelling in ICU sepsis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13661)
*Yawen Zhang,Wanzhe Xu,Zhenhua Zhang,Jinyu Wu,Yizhou Zhuang,Yitao Yang,Kenta Nakai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SepsisFlow 是一个以入院后小时级时间轴为统一框架的脓毒症全程模型：预警前用预训练 Mamba 结合梯度提升树预测 6/12/24 小时风险，发病时以门控网络融合多模态编码生成"发病情境"患者状态，发病后以注意力双向循环网络刻画 72 小时早期病程轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脓毒症是 ICU 主要死因且起病与病程异质性强，但现有预警模型多只预测"是否会发生"，报警后临床医生难以判断发病情境与早期演变轨迹，缺乏统一时钟下连续的风险与轨迹刻画工具。

Method: 构建三阶段统一小时级建模：发病前将预训练于 257 万份 EHR 的 Mamba 模型与约 1700 个逐小时特征的梯度提升树结合，输出 6/12/24 小时风险；发病时刻通过门控网络融合 Mamba 与表格编码器得到连续患者状态以定义发病情境；发病后以注意力双向循环网络编码 72 小时器官支持与生理轨迹，形成早期病程表征。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV 的发病前预警中，同等报警次数下检出病例数多于 NEWS2、qSOFA、SIRS，小时级更新每 100 例约多检出 8 例，并优于 16 种重训练算法；在 eICU 中保持风险排序能力。发病情境可区分 2%（心血管 ICU）与 15%–17%（其他）的 28 天死亡率；同一情境内，发病后持续器官支持者的死亡率约为降阶梯者的 5 倍。

Conclusion: SepsisFlow 为重症监护中的连续风险预测与轨迹分层提供了基础模型范式，将预警、情境识别与早期病程刻画统一于单一时间框架。

Abstract: Sepsis is a leading cause of ICU death with heterogeneous onset and course, yet most warning models predict only whether sepsis will occur, leaving clinicians uncertain of its onset context and early trajectory once an alarm sounds. To answer these questions on one hourly clock from ICU admission, we built SepsisFlow: before onset, it combines a Mamba model pretrained on 2.57 million EHRs with about 1,700 hourly features in gradient-boosted trees to predict 6/12/24-h risk; at onset, it fuses Mamba and tabular encoders via a gated network into continuous patient states defining onset contexts; after onset, it encodes 72-h organ-support and physiological trajectories via an attention bidirectional recurrent network into early courses. For pre-onset warning in MIMIC-IV, SepsisFlow detected more cases than NEWS2, qSOFA, and SIRS at equal alarms, gaining about 8 cases per 100 from hourly updating, while outperforming 16 retrained algorithms and maintaining risk ranking in eICU. At onset, its contexts separated 2% cardiovascular-ICU from 15%-17% other 28-day mortality, and within each context after onset, persistent support carried fivefold higher mortality than de-escalation. SepsisFlow establishes a foundation-model paradigm for continuous risk and trajectory stratification in critical care.

</details>


### [195] [Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13680)
*Yue Wu,Xueyu Zhou,Yangrong Hu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)在持续训练下会因特权教师与无参考学生之间的分布失配而性能退化，据此提出两阶段框架BOPD，先在中间模型上内化参考条件监督，再将其蒸馏到新学生模型，在数学推理与代码生成上优于初始模型和OPSD教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD早期收益显著，但持续训练后效果不稳定甚至显著退化；作者将退化归因于不断演化的无参考学生与冻结的特权教师之间日益增大的分布失配，此时教师会提供误导性监督。

Method: 提出Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation (BOPD)两阶段框架：第一阶段用OPSD将参考条件（特权）监督中的任务相关信息内化到一个中间模型；第二阶段将该无参考中间模型蒸馏到一个初始模型的全新副本中。

Result: 跨多个模型的实验表明，BOPD在数学推理和代码生成任务上均超过初始模型以及所构建的OPSD教师模型。

Conclusion: 特权参考监督更有效的利用方式是：先将其任务相关信号迁移到一个任务适配模型，再从该模型（无参考条件）进行蒸馏，而非直接长期依赖特权教师。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language models by distilling teacher predictions on student-generated trajectories, while on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) removes the need for an external teacher by using a reference-conditioned copy of the model as the teacher. Despite its great early improvements, we find that OPSD does not remain consistently effective under continued training and can eventually suffer substantial performance degradation. We study this training behavior and link the degradation to a growing mismatch between the evolving reference-free student and the frozen privileged teacher. In the early stage of OPSD, the privileged teacher remains closely aligned with the student and provides useful guidance for improving the student's generation trajectories. However, as training progresses, the growing distributional mismatch between the privileged teacher and reference-free student can cause the teacher to provide misleading supervision, ultimately degrading student performance. This phenomenon motivates us to first leverage privileged reference supervision to acquire useful knowledge and then transfer it to a fresh student. We introduce Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation (BOPD), a two-stage framework that first uses OPSD to internalize task-relevant information from reference-conditioned supervision into an intermediate model, and then distills this reference-free intermediate model into a fresh copy of the initial model. Experiments across models show that BOPD improves mathematical reasoning and code generation beyond both the initial models and the constructed OPSD teachers. These results suggest that privileged reference supervision can be more effectively exploited by first transferring its task-relevant signal into a task-adapted model and then distilling from that model without reference conditioning.

</details>


### [196] [FedVP: Federated Learning across Heterogeneous Models by Pooling Locally Trained Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13708)
*Yifan Li,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦验证器池（FEDVP），用"共享验证"替代参数聚合，使异构LLM客户端可在冻结骨干前提下协作提升答案选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖共享模型骨干进行客户端更新聚合，要求同构架构；这限制了使用不同家族、不同规模LLM的机构之间的协作。

Method: 每个客户端用本地数据及其所选LLM骨干生成的回答训练一个验证器；服务器汇集独立训练的验证器，组合其判断来选择答案，而不合并任何模型参数。LLM骨干保持冻结，骨干与验证器在架构和规模上均可不同。

Result: 在五个公开医学问答数据集（作为联邦客户端）上，使用3B–14B参数的Qwen与LLaMA骨干，FEDVP在全部五个客户端上均稳定提升性能，平均准确率较最强传统联邦基线高出1.46–5.79个百分点。

Conclusion: FEDVP表明"验证器共享"可作为参数聚合之外的可行联邦范式，在支持强异构性的同时获得协作增益；但其收益限于答案选择层面，骨干能力本身未被增强。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning relies on the shared model backbone to aggregate client updates, limiting collaboration among institutions that deploy large language models (LLMs) from different families and at different scales. We propose Federated Verifier Pool (FEDVP), a new federated learning paradigm that enables heterogeneous clients to collaborate through shared verification rather than parameter aggregation. Each client trains a verifier using local data and responses generated by its chosen LLM backbone. The server pools these independently trained verifiers and combines their judgments to select answers without merging model parameters. This allows both LLM backbones and verifiers to differ in architecture and size, while keeping the LLM backbones frozen and deriving collaborative gains from improved answer selection. We evaluate FEDVP across five public medical question-answering datasets treated as federated clients, using Qwen and LLaMA backbones ranging from 3B to 14B parameters. FEDVP consistently improves performance across all five clients and outperforms the strongest conventional federated baselines by 1.46-5.79 percentage points in mean accuracy.

</details>


### [197] [TRUER: Test-Time Reward Correction Using an Error-Aware Rectifier for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13714)
*Mingxiang Shen,Jinhao Li,Jiaming Xu,Yibo Han,Guohao Dai,Yijia Zhang,Ningyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRUER：用少量有标签数据训练奖励矫正器，让测试时强化学习区分“共识”与“正确”，抑制多数投票带来的假阳性奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习依赖模型自身答案导出的奖励，但多数投票会在存在正确解时仍强化错误解。训练审计显示，假阳性（错误回答获得正奖励）是奖励误差的主要来源；同时大规模获取真值奖励不现实，仅有有限标注源集可用。

Method: 利用有限监督训练奖励矫正器，重点在“错误多数”与“正确少数”样本上训练，专门针对共识与正确性不一致的情形；训练完成后冻结该矫正器，在后续测试时自适应中以它校正奖励。

Result: 通过提升奖励准确率进而改善模型表现，TRUER在所有评估配置下于两项指标上均稳定优于朴素TTRL。

Conclusion: 以少量标注监督矫正奖励信号，是缓解测试时强化学习中假阳性奖励的有效途径，可将“共识”与“正确”解耦，在无大规模真值奖励的场景下稳定超越基线。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning enables language models to adapt using rewards derived from their own answers. However, majority voting can reinforce incorrect solutions even when correct alternatives are available. Our training audit shows that false positives (incorrect responses receiving positive rewards) dominate reward errors. We focus on a practical regime where ground-truth rewards are difficult to obtain at scale, while a limited labeled source set is available. We introduce TRUER, a framework that uses limited supervision to train a reward rectifier that distinguishes correctness from consensus. To achieve this goal, we train the rectifier with an emphasis on incorrect majorities and correct minorities, targeting cases where consensus and correctness disagree. After that, we freeze the rectifier and use it to correct rewards during subsequent test-time adaptation. By improving reward accuracy and thereby model performance, TRUER consistently outperforms naive TTRL in both metrics across all evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [198] [Weighted-AoI-Driven UAV Trajectory and Reporting for Dynamic CKM Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13726)
*Zeyu Zhao,Yueling Che,Sheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于加权信息年龄（W-AoI）的无人机机队在线动态信道知识地图（CKM）跟踪方法，通过2D U-Net预测空间不确定性与贪心轨迹策略，显著降低稳态误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CKM构建假设准静态环境，信道快速去相关时失效；需在无人机移动与上报约束下实现在线动态跟踪，但未测量小区的误差不可观测，导致长期误差最小化问题难以求解。

Method: 将问题建模为无人机机队在空中2D平面上的长期误差最小化；提出以W-AoI为驱动指标的调度方法，融合精确时间陈旧度与2D U-Net预测的空间不确定性；证明top-K W-AoI选择可最大化单时隙误差下降上界；据此设计轻量贪心轨迹策略并结合沿路径连续感知。

Result: 仿真表明稳态误差较仅AoI与FIFO基线分别降低3.8 dB²和4.8 dB²，并接近理想oracle性能。

Conclusion: W-AoI调度在保证误差下降界的前提下实现高效动态CKM跟踪，为无人机辅助的在线信道映射提供可行且接近最优的方案。

Abstract: Existing channel knowledge map (CKM) construction assumes quasi-static environments, failing when channels rapidly decorrelate. This paper investigates continuous online dynamic CKM tracking over an airborne 2D plane via a UAV fleet. We formulate a long-term error minimization problem under UAV mobility and reporting constraints. This problem, however, is intractable with unobservable errors for unmeasured cells. To tackle this, we propose a metric-driven scheduling approach featuring the Weighted-Age of Information (W-AoI). W-AoI evaluates cells by combining exact temporal staleness with spatial uncertainty predicted by a 2D U-Net. We prove that top-$K$ W-AoI selection maximizes the one-slot error reduction bound. Guided by this guarantee, we develop a lightweight greedy trajectory policy incorporating continuous along-path sensing. Simulations demonstrate our method reduces steady-state error by 3.8 dB$^2$ and 4.8 dB$^2$ over AoI-only and FIFO baselines, closely approaching an idealized oracle.

</details>


### [199] [Deterministic, Reproducible Inference in Exact Integer Arithmetic, and Verifiable Distributed Inference as Its Consequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13730)
*Sayed Amir Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: tritify：将大模型前向推理全程置于精确整数运算，实现跨硬件位级可复现、决策保真、位置无关的分布式可验证推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮点优化推理在不同硬件、编译器、线程数下不可复现（同模型产出不同比特）。作者希望获得可被独立复算、可验证的推理路径，从而支撑分布式与不信任环境下的推理。

Method: 开源运行时 tritify：解码器-only LM、MoE 解码器、SAM 2.1 图像/视频栈的前向全部用精确整数算术执行，浮点仅用于离线量化与验证用浮点参考。提出并逐项验证三项声明：(1) 跨硬件可复现（同机多次、跨 ISA、跨 CPU/GPU 位级一致，以输出 SHA-256 复现佐证）；(2) 决策保真（与浮点参考决策一致，测量数值例外；OLMoE-1B-7B 与 106B MoE 十六层逐层保真无"保真悬崖"）；(3) 位置无关（计算可跨 ISA、CPU/GPU、联网机器切分而不改变输出比特）。

Result: GLM-4.5-Air（106,852,251,264 参数、427 GB 整数码）跨三台机器生成答案，任一台均无法在 RAM 容纳其整数码，输出与单机参考位级一致；十六层逐层保真无悬崖。

Conclusion: 实现可验证的分布式推理：不可信服务器或设备群运行模型，开放标量 oracle 重新推导精确字节。局限：不保证对不诚实服务器的防护（未来工作）、不声称单设备加速、浮点中间值的位相等或机密性。

Abstract: Optimized floating-point inference is not reproducible across environments: the same model on different hardware, compilers or thread counts can produce different bits. We present tritify, an open-source runtime that executes the forward pass of real models -- decoder-only language models, Mixture-of-Experts decoders, and the Segment Anything 2.1 image/video stack -- with the entire run-time path in exact integer arithmetic; floating point is confined to offline quantization and the float reference used for verification. We make three separate claims and verify each. (1) Cross-hardware reproducibility: the integer path produces bit-identical output run to run, across CPU instruction-set architectures, and across CPU/GPU, carried by a reproducing SHA-256 of the output. (2) Decision fidelity: the integer path reproduces the float reference's decision on the models and inputs tested, with the numeric exceptions measured; per-layer fidelity through sixteen layers of OLMoE-1B-7B and of a 106-billion-parameter MoE shows no fidelity cliff. (3) Location independence: a model's computation can be split across instruction sets, CPU and GPU, and networked machines with zero effect on the output bits. GLM-4.5-Air (106,852,251,264 parameters, 427 GB of integer codes) generated its answer across three machines, none of which can hold its integer codes in RAM, bit-identically to a single-host reference. The consequence is verifiable distributed inference: an untrusted server or a fleet of devices runs the model while an open scalar oracle re-derives the exact bytes; a guarantee against a dishonest server is future work. We do not claim a per-device speed-up, bit-equality of floating-valued intermediates, or confidentiality. Code: https://github.com/pelloran-inc/tritify

</details>


### [200] [Plug-and-Play ADMM for Inverse Problems with Flow Matching Denoiser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13742)
*Dibyanshu Kumar,Magda Gregorova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于ADMM的即插即用(PnP)框架，将flow matching生成先验与测量模型通过辅助变量和对偶更新强化一致性，并用确定性+随机投影的轨迹校正机制恢复flow轨迹一致性，实现高质量且低计算成本的重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PnP方法中数据保真更新会使迭代点偏离flow轨迹，导致数据保真步骤与学习到的生成先验之间一致性难以维持，这是PnP方法的核心挑战。

Method: 1) 构建ADMM-based PnP框架用于flow matching先验，借助辅助变量与对偶更新在测量模型与先验间施加更强一致性约束；2) 引入轨迹校正机制，通过确定性投影与随机投影的组合将迭代点拉回flow轨迹。

Result: 实现了flow先验在PnP框架中更原理化的集成，在逆问题重建上取得高质量结果，同时降低计算开销。

Conclusion: 基于ADMM的一致性强化与轨迹校正为flow matching先验融入PnP逆问题提供了更严谨的范式，兼具重建质量与计算效率优势。

Abstract: Plug-and-Play (PnP) methods with generative priors have achieved strong performance in inverse problems, with recent flow matching models enabling fast and scalable reconstruction. However, a key challenge in PnP methods is maintaining consistency between data fidelity updates and the learned generative prior, as data-consistency steps can move iterates away from the flow trajectory. We propose an ADMM-based PnP framework for flow matching priors that enforces stronger consistency between the measurement model and the prior through auxiliary variables and dual updates. In addition, we introduce a trajectory correction mechanism that restores consistency with the flow via a combination of deterministic and stochastic projections. Our method provides a more principled integration of flow priors within PnP frameworks, achieving high-quality reconstructions with reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [201] [When Covariance Is Not Enough: Kernel-Based Regularization for Single-Cell Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13748)
*Korbinian Traeuble,Edward de Brouwer,Archit Verma,Doron Haviv,Kipper Fletez-Brant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出线性交叉协方差惩罚无法在存在非线性交互时完全解耦已知识与未知因子，提出在概念瓶颈生成模型中加入归一化希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（NHSIC）作为非线性核准则，从而更忠实地实现单细胞反事实编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞反事实编辑只有在编辑对个体（而非仅对条件）忠实时才有价值。概念瓶颈生成模型通过将细胞表示分解为已知因子与未知因子使干预效应可解释，但以往用线性交叉协方差惩罚做解耦，作者认为该一阶准则在存在非线性交互时无法充分分离信息，因此需要非线性的解耦准则。

Method: 先通过带有受控编辑和已知非线性协变量效应的仿真实验，说明线性交叉因子惩罚的不足；再在概念瓶颈生成模型中加入非线性核准则——归一化希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（NHSIC）替代/补充线性判据；最后将该非线性概念瓶颈模型应用于复杂单细胞RNA-seq数据集。

Result: 仿真实验表明线性交叉协方差惩罚在非线性情形下分离信息不充分；加入NHSIC后能够在仿真数据上恢复真实的干预效应；在真实单细胞RNA-seq数据上，非线性概念瓶颈模型提升了在不同组织环境下生成细胞反事实表达谱的能力。

Conclusion: 非线性核独立性准则（NHSIC）优于线性交叉协方差惩罚，可有效解耦已知与未知因子，带来更忠实、更准确的单细胞反事实编辑，并在复杂真实数据上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Counterfactual editing of single cells, predicting how an observed cell would look had one factor been different, enables in-silico screening where experiments are infeasible, but is useful only if the edit is faithful to the individual and not merely to the condition. Concept bottleneck generative models make the effects of interventions interpretable by decomposing a cell's representation into known and unknown factors. Previous work uses a linear cross-covariance penalty to disentangle known and unknown dimensions. We demonstrate that this first-order criterion does not fully separate information when non-linear interactions are present. We first demonstrate the inadequacy of linear cross-factor penalization with simulation experiments with controlled edits and known non-linear covariate effects. We then demonstrate that adding a nonlinear kernel criterion, the normalized Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion, is able to recover the true intervention effects on simulation data. Finally, we apply the non-linear concept bottleneck model to a complex single-cell RNA-seq dataset and find that it improves the ability to generate counterfactual expression profiles of cells under different tissue environments.

</details>


### [202] [Mixed Signals: Weight-Space Programming for EEG and ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13758)
*Dara Varam,Taraneh Aminosharieh Najafi,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把"权重空间编程"（让语言模型直接读取并编辑神经网络权重）推广到生理信号模型（EEG/ECG），证明前沿模型能在不被告知故障位置的情况下调试植入的具体故障，并能仅凭架构、任务与极少训练被试从零写出全部权重；仅用5名被试写出的网络即超过经典手工EEG管线，且以6倍更少被试达到梯度下降训练水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间编程为"通过推理而非梯度下降来训练"提供了可能，此前已在博弈网络、语言模型特定失效修复乃至扩散模型上验证，但尚未在生理信号（EEG/ECG）这类低信噪比、跨被试差异大、常部署于边缘与可穿戴设备的小型模型上考察其适用性。

Method: 以EEG/ECG训练的网络为对象，向语言模型提供网络权重、架构、任务描述与极少量训练被试信息，让其(1)在不给定故障信息的前提下定位并修复植入的特定故障；(2)完全通过推理、无任何梯度下降步骤地从零写出网络全部权重；并与标准梯度下降训练及经典手工特征EEG管线（alpha频带功率、CSP+LDA）在相同被试下对比。同时总结失败模式作为局限分析。

Result: 语言模型仅用约1/6的训练被试即匹配标准梯度下降训练的效果；由Claude Opus 5.5写出的网络在相同5名被试条件下得分高于alpha频带功率与CSP+LDA等经典手工EEG管线；同时模型无法改进已训练良好的分类模型，也难以仅凭权重可靠识别网络所训练的任务。

Conclusion: 权重空间编程对边缘设备与可穿戴设备上的小型网络具有实用潜力，可作为梯度下降的补充范式，但其能力边界（不能提升良好模型、难以由权重反推任务）表明该概念仍处早期，投入实际应用前需进一步研究。

Abstract: Weight-space programming allows language models to read a trained neural network's weights and edit them. This opens the door to ``training'' by reasoning rather than through gradient descent. Prior work has demonstrated its functionality and promise for game-playing networks and fixing specific failures in language models, even extending to diffusion models. In this work, we present its applicability to models trained on biosignal data, specifically EEG and ECG data. We show that frontier models are not only able to debug specific faults planted in biosignal-based models without being told what these faults are, but are even capable of writing all the weights of a network from scratch, given only the architecture, the task, and a minimal set of training subjects. In particular, in one set of experiments, a language model was able to match standard gradient-descent training using $6\times$ fewer training subjects, through reasoning alone and without any gradient descent step. Networks written by Claude Opus 5.5 also scored higher than classic hand-crafted EEG pipelines (alpha band power, and common spatial patterns with linear discriminant analysis) when built from the same 5 subjects. We also highlight some of the limitations, which include the inability of language models to improve already well-trained classification models, and their incapacity to reliably recognize, from the weights alone, the task a network was trained on. Overall, our results indicate that weight-space programming can be useful for small networks, such as those deployed on edge devices and wearables, but the concept still requires further investigation before it can be used in practice.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Interaction Scores: Selecting Feature Interactions via Independent Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13765)
*Jincheng Gao,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LENS 是一个模型无关的特征交互选择框架，通过独立重复检验在全量候选对中筛选交互，仅需两次模型拟合，具备选择一致性理论保证，并在合成数据、保险与信贷数据上优于或持平现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征交互对准确预测与可解释建模至关重要，但高交互得分可能只是拟合噪声而非可复现模式。现有排序类方法（如 TreeSHAP）只给分不设阈值，需用户自定截断；检验类方法（如 knockoff 系）虽能设阈值，却依赖重复重拟合或对联合特征分布的建模假设，代价高且受限。

Method: LENS 在同一学习算法下于不相交数据上训练两个模型，仅通过预测访问模型。对每个特征对，从联合效应面中剔除主效应以隔离交互模式，按其强度排序，并检验第二个模型是否在具意义强度上复现该模式；再用单次多重检验校正决定保留哪些对，拟合次数恒为2，与特征数无关。理论部分证明仅用复现比例会保留任意弱的共同模式，加入强度下限可恢复选择一致性，并刻画 Friedman H² 中归一化何时会逆转交互排序。

Result: 16 组合成设计上，LENS 与 TreeSHAP 等主流模型特定排序方法相当，回收 91% 的真实交互（knockoff 类 DIAMOND 为 45%），且不选出假对；在 1,225 个候选对且无交互情形下假发现显著更少。大规模法国车险数据上识别出精算文献记载的交互，并以 90% 的比例回收全对模型的偏差增益。两个信贷数据集上仅选少量对，预测性能持平或略优于可加基线。

Conclusion: LENS 提供了低成本（两次拟合）、模型无关且具统计保证的交互选择方案，兼顾可复现性与选择精度，在理论与实证上均优于排序法与 knockoff 类检验法，可作为可解释建模中交互筛选的通用工具。

Abstract: Learning feature interactions is essential for accurate prediction and interpretable modeling, yet a high interaction score may reflect fitting noise rather than a reproducible pattern. Ranking methods score feature pairs but leave the cutoff to the user; testing methods set the cutoff but rely on repeated model refits or a model of the joint feature distribution. We present LENS, a model-agnostic framework for selecting feature interactions through independent replication. LENS trains two models with the same learning algorithm on disjoint data and accesses them only through predictions.
